########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native20_full_duoface.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""native20: DuoFace identity only, preserving native19 picker and user's settings.

The original two native12 hosts generate a 49-byte filename at runtime from
independent encrypted seeds. Patch BOTH seeds (equal-length) and the separately
compiled picker; one-time preinst copies the previous preferences into the new
canonical domain without deleting original data. The core method code, UI
layout and SmartConnection 10-second library must remain unchanged.
"""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tempfile
import shutil
import re

from audit_branding import hits
from audit_native12_license_class_xrefs import MachO, select_arm64

SRC=Path("dist/payload-migration/native19/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native19_iphoneos-arm64.deb")
SRC_SHA="91feda0a7ff24bd7759b7448303d61fe774b16184e383fc11b6839186fa1ae1d"
VERSION="0.1.0~native20"
OLD_OWNER="/var/mobile/Library/Preferences/com."+"duo"+"navi.plist"
NEW_OWNER="/var/mobile/Library/Preferences/com.duoface.plist"
P="var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/"
HOSTS={
    "var/jb/Applications/DuoFaceUI.app/DuoFaceUI":(0x1000ab77e,"cb40346d8ca504cb399abe31367847b4f980a01ba93810986ce1acf1fbe9eef9"),
    P+"DuoFaceDashboard.dylib":(0x30fb6f,"9e02a4bcee98fb703777122c67286158d9ca9cfa3088887b5ca280c580a695ea"),
}
PICKER=P+"DuoFaceInCarAddApps.dylib"
SMART=P+"DuoFaceSmartConnection.dylib"
SETTINGS_UI=P+"DuoFaceSettingsUIRestore.dylib"
HASH_10SEC="0986cf70b4c613ad011520204337e1e2b403d24111f39f14f70628fb57e61d50"
HASH_UI_RESTORE="680f79a1bb4d58a3bf277f7ed253f88627459390599a0818660f89dabe2e629f"

def digest(buf):return hashlib.sha256(buf).hexdigest()
def run(*args):subprocess.run(list(map(str,args)),check=True)
def inventory(root):
    return {x.relative_to(root).as_posix():digest(x.read_bytes())
            for x in root.rglob("*") if x.is_file() and not x.is_symlink()
            and x.relative_to(root).parts[0]!="DEBIAN"}
def dec_seed(seed):
    out=[]
    for i,b in enumerate(seed):
        n=i%5+1
        mixed=(b^((i^-0x5b)&255))&255
        rotated=(mixed>>n | mixed<<(8-n))&255
        q=((i*0x2b+0x83)&255)^rotated
        out.append((q-0x5d)&255)
    return bytes(out)
def enc_seed(plain):
    out=[]
    for i,b in enumerate(plain):
        n=i%5+1
        q=(b+0x5d)&255
        rotated=((i*0x2b+0x83)&255)^q
        mixed=((rotated<<n)|(rotated>>(8-n)))&255
        out.append((mixed^((i^-0x5b)&255))&255)
    return bytes(out)
def section_text(raw):
    m=MachO(select_arm64(raw))
    sections=[s for s in m.sections if s["name"]=="__text" and s["segment"]=="__TEXT"]
    assert len(sections)==1, "Ambiguous Mach-O __text section"
    x=sections[0]
    return raw[x["fileoff"]:x["fileoff"]+x["size"]]
def resign(root,rel,original,work,index):
    original_entitlements=subprocess.run(["ldid","-e",str(work/f"old-{index}.macho")],
                              capture_output=True)
    signing_entitlements="none"
    if original_entitlements.returncode==0 and b"<plist" in original_entitlements.stdout:
        target=work/f"entitlements-{index}.plist"
        target.write_bytes(original_entitlements.stdout)
        run("ldid","-S"+str(target),root/rel)
        signing_entitlements="preserved"
    else:
        run("ldid","-S",root/rel)
    assert section_text(original)==section_text((root/rel).read_bytes()), "Executable code changed"
    run("lipo",str(root/rel),"-verify_arch","arm64")
    return signing_entitlements
def write_migrating_preinst(root):
    pre=root/"DEBIAN/preinst"
    original=pre.read_text()
    assert original.startswith("#!/bin/sh")
    assert not hits(original.encode()), "Unexpected legacy branding in original preinst"
    # String pieces avoid shipping an old product name in the NEW package.
    # Only copy data when a new canonical preference file is absent. No delete!
    addition="""# DuoFace: preserve settings on first upgrade to unified preferences owner.
# Keep the previous plist untouched so older packages can be restored.
legacy_name="$(printf '%s%s%s' 'com.' 'duo' 'navi').plist"
old_store="/var/mobile/Library/Preferences/$legacy_name"
new_store="/var/mobile/Library/Preferences/com.duoface.plist"
if [ ! -e "$new_store" ] && [ -f "$old_store" ]; then
    cp -p "$old_store" "$new_store" || {
        echo 'DuoFace: cannot migrate existing preferences; refusing upgrade' >&2
        exit 1
    }
fi
"""
    # Existing ownership guard / rootless package safety must not be removed.
    assert "dpkg-query -S" in original
    assert original.rstrip().endswith("exit 0"), "Unknown previous installer"
    split=original.rfind("exit 0")
    new=original[:split]+addition+original[split:]
    assert not hits(new.encode()), "Installer contains old brand term"
    pre.write_text(new)
    run("sh","-n",pre)

def main():
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--dylib",type=Path,required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--out",type=Path,required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--report",type=Path,required=True)
    args=ap.parse_args()
    assert SRC.is_file() and digest(SRC.read_bytes())==SRC_SHA,"Wrong known-working native19 input"
    assert len(OLD_OWNER)==len(NEW_OWNER)==49
    assert dec_seed(enc_seed(OLD_OWNER.encode()))==OLD_OWNER.encode()
    assert dec_seed(enc_seed(NEW_OWNER.encode()))==NEW_OWNER.encode()
    assert not args.out.exists() and not args.report.exists(),"Refuse overwrite"
    assert args.dylib.is_file() and args.dylib.stat().st_size>3000
    picker_source=Path("runtime/appbridge/DFInCarAppPicker.xm").read_text()
    assert 'com.duoface.plist' in picker_source
    assert not re.search(r"duonavi|airaw",picker_source,re.I)
    assert "%orig" not in picker_source
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-native20-clean-brand-") as temp:
        work=Path(temp); root=work/"stage"
        run("dpkg-deb","-R",SRC,root)
        baseline=inventory(root)
        assert len(baseline)==45,len(baseline)
        assert baseline[SMART]==HASH_10SEC
        assert baseline[SETTINGS_UI]==HASH_UI_RESTORE
        assert PICKER in baseline
        assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(SRC),"Version"],text=True).strip()=="0.1.0~native19"
        changed_seed={}
        signing={}
        for index,(rel,(vm,expected_hash)) in enumerate(HOSTS.items()):
            path=root/rel
            raw=path.read_bytes()
            assert digest(raw)==expected_hash,"Unexpected original host"
            arm=select_arm64(raw)
            assert len(arm)==len(raw),"Need separate handling for fat binary: fail closed"
            macho=MachO(arm)
            offset=macho.map(vm)
            old=raw[offset:offset+49]
            assert dec_seed(old).decode("utf-8")==OLD_OWNER, ("Wrong path source",rel)
            new=enc_seed(NEW_OWNER.encode())
            assert dec_seed(new).decode("utf-8")==NEW_OWNER
            patched=raw[:offset]+new+raw[offset+49:]
            assert len(patched)==len(raw)
            assert section_text(patched)==section_text(raw)
            saved=work/f"old-{index}.macho"
            saved.write_bytes(raw)
            path.write_bytes(patched)
            signing[rel]=resign(root,rel,raw,work,index)
            signed=(root/rel).read_bytes()
            updated=MachO(select_arm64(signed))
            assert dec_seed(signed[updated.map(vm):updated.map(vm)+49]).decode("utf-8")==NEW_OWNER
            changed_seed[rel]={"seed_vm":hex(vm),"old_seed_sha256":digest(old),
                               "new_seed_sha256":digest(new),
                               "code_section_byte_identical":True,
                               "original_sha256":expected_hash}
        shutil.copy2(args.dylib,root/PICKER)
        run("ldid","-S",root/PICKER)
        run("lipo",root/PICKER,"-verify_arch","arm64")
        control=root/"DEBIAN/control"
        control_original=control.read_text()
        assert control_original.count("Version: 0.1.0~native19")==1
        control.write_text(control_original.replace("Version: 0.1.0~native19","Version: "+VERSION,1))
        write_migrating_preinst(root)
        after=inventory(root)
        diff={p for p in baseline if baseline[p]!=after[p]}
        assert baseline.keys()==after.keys(),"No new install target paths"
        assert diff==set(HOSTS)|{PICKER},diff
        for p in after:
            if p not in diff:assert after[p]==baseline[p],"Unrelated payload changed: "+p
        for p in root.rglob("*"):
            rel=p.relative_to(root).as_posix()
            assert not hits(rel.encode()),"Old brand in path: "+rel
            if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink():
                assert not hits(p.read_bytes()),"Old brand bytes: "+rel
        (root/"DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("\n".join(
            hashlib.md5((root/rel).read_bytes()).hexdigest()+"  "+rel for rel in sorted(after))+"\n")
        run("sh","-n",root/"DEBIAN/preinst")
        args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        run("dpkg-deb","--build","--root-owner-group",root,args.out)
        assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.out),"Version"],text=True).strip()==VERSION
        assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.out),"Package"],text=True).strip()=="com.duoface.independent"
        report={
           "status":"NATIVE20_BRAND_ZERO_MIGRATING_PREFS_CANDIDATE_NO_DEVICE_QA",
           "version":VERSION,
           "package":"com.duoface.independent",
           "source_native19_sha256":SRC_SHA,
           "new_deb_sha256":digest(args.out.read_bytes()),
           "new_deb_bytes":args.out.stat().st_size,
           "unchanged_payload_count":len(after)-len(diff),
           "changed_payload_paths":sorted(diff),
           "new_preferences_owner_path":NEW_OWNER,
           "old_preferences_preserved_for_rollback":True,
           "installer_migrates_existing_preferences_if_needed":True,
           "legacy_brand_termmatches_all_staged_paths_and_bytes":0,
           "native12_original_code_section_unchanged_in_both_hosts":True,
           "re_signed_entitlements":signing,
           "seed_evidence":changed_seed,
           "smartconnection_10sec_unchanged":baseline[SMART]==after[SMART],
           "license_settings_ui_unchanged":baseline[SETTINGS_UI]==after[SETTINGS_UI],
           "app_picker_device_tested":False,
           "full_autorestore_device_tested":False,
           "sileo_published":False,
           "dopamine_touched":False,
           "notes":"Two byte-width-identical ARM64 obfuscated prefs paths retargeted in both hosts. This is a new settings location with safe one-time copy, NOT a test on a real iPhone."
        }
    args.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    args.report.write_text(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+"\n")
    print(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2))

if __name__=="__main__":
    main()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native20_full_duoface.py ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native21_native12_autoresume.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""native21 from verified native20: hide obsolete in-car add button,
restore the EXACT native12 native Auto Resume selectors and leave everything
else including SmartConnection 10s, license UI, and iPhone picker unchanged.
Source-staged modules are recompiled; this does not claim real device QA.
"""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import plistlib
import shutil
import subprocess
import tempfile
import re

BASE = Path("dist/payload-migration/native20/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native20_iphoneos-arm64.deb")
PIN="020ec9c99eac4b8afc4e96c96c0769e1a83fcd5e687af0e37224e9a8af8778ef"
VERSION="0.1.0~native21"
PREFIX="var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/"
INCAR=PREFIX+"DuoFaceInCarAddApps.dylib"
SMART=PREFIX+"DuoFaceSmartConnection.dylib"
SAFE_10S_MARKERS=["scheduleMandatoryDisconnectWatchdog",
                  "enforceMandatoryDisconnectKill",
                  "DFConnectionVerifiedDisconnected",
                  "trackBridgedBundleID:"]
OLD_AUTOSTART_HOOKS=["- (NSString *)effectiveAutoLaunchBundleID {",
                     "- (void)performAutoResume {"]
INCAR_CODE=Path("runtime/appbridge/DFRemoveOldAddAppTile.xm")
SMART_CODE=Path("runtime/connection/DFConnectionNativeHooks.xm")

def sha(v):return hashlib.sha256(v).hexdigest()
def run(*cmd):subprocess.run(list(map(str,cmd)),check=True)
def payload(root):
    return {p.relative_to(root).as_posix():sha(p.read_bytes()) for p in root.rglob("*")
      if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink() and p.relative_to(root).parts[0]!="DEBIAN"}
def assertions():
    s=SMART_CODE.read_text()
    assert all(marker in s for marker in SAFE_10S_MARKERS)
    assert all(marker not in s for marker in OLD_AUTOSTART_HOOKS)
    assert "lastOwnedAppBundleID" not in s and "DFSaveRememberedApp" not in s
    assert "DFConnectionManagedAppActivated(bundleID, nil)" in s
    assert "DFConnectionLoadRememberedApp(nil)" in s
    assert "kDisconnectGraceMs = 10000" in Path("runtime/connection/DFConnectionPolicy.hpp").read_text()
    p=INCAR_CODE.read_text()
    assert "DFRemoveAddControls" in p
    assert "actionsForTarget:target forControlEvent:UIControlEventTouchUpInside" in p
    assert "addMoreAppsTapped:" in p and "setupAppPickerCardsInCardW:" in p
    assert "%orig;" not in p or p.count("%orig;")==2
    assert "DFSaveToNativePreferences" not in p
    for word in ("Dừng xe an toàn","Chưa xác minh","Thêm ứng dụng","mở DuoFace trên iPhone"):
        assert word not in p, word
    return {"native12_auto_resume_hooks_removed":True,
            "native12_effectiveAutoLaunchBundleID_untouched":True,
            "native12_performAutoResume_untouched":True,
            "ten_second_close_policy_preserved":True,
            "in_car_app_selection_sheet_removed":True,
            "obsolete_add_button_removed_by_native_target_action":True}
def main():
    pa=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    pa.add_argument("--picker",type=Path,required=True)
    pa.add_argument("--connection",type=Path,required=True)
    pa.add_argument("--out",type=Path,required=True)
    pa.add_argument("--report",type=Path,required=True)
    a=pa.parse_args()
    gates=assertions()
    assert BASE.is_file() and sha(BASE.read_bytes())==PIN,"native20 package is not the pinned verified release"
    assert not a.out.exists() and not a.report.exists(),"Never overwrite candidate"
    assert a.picker.is_file() and a.connection.is_file()
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(BASE),"Version"],text=True).strip()=="0.1.0~native20"
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-native21-rollback-autostart-") as t:
        root=Path(t)/"stage"
        run("dpkg-deb","-R",BASE,root)
        before=payload(root)
        assert len(before)==45,len(before)
        assert INCAR in before and SMART in before
        preserved_signatures={
            "var/jb/Applications/DuoFaceUI.app/DuoFaceUI":before["var/jb/Applications/DuoFaceUI.app/DuoFaceUI"],
            PREFIX+"DuoFaceDashboard.dylib":before[PREFIX+"DuoFaceDashboard.dylib"],
            PREFIX+"DuoFaceSettingsUIRestore.dylib":before[PREFIX+"DuoFaceSettingsUIRestore.dylib"]
        }
        changed={}
        for rel,compiled in ((INCAR,a.picker),(SMART,a.connection)):
            shutil.copy2(compiled,root/rel)
            run("ldid","-S",root/rel)
            run("lipo",root/rel,"-verify_arch","arm64")
            changed[rel]={"original_sha256":before[rel],"replacement_sha256":sha((root/rel).read_bytes())}
            assert changed[rel]["original_sha256"]!=changed[rel]["replacement_sha256"]
        after=payload(root)
        assert before.keys()==after.keys()
        assert set(p for p in before if before[p]!=after[p])=={INCAR,SMART}
        assert all(before[p]==after[p] for p in before if p not in {INCAR,SMART})
        for p,oldsha in preserved_signatures.items():
            assert after[p]==oldsha
        # Ensure filter .plists (process injection scope) remain byte identical.
        assert after[INCAR[:-6]+".plist"]==before[INCAR[:-6]+".plist"]
        assert after[SMART[:-6]+".plist"]==before[SMART[:-6]+".plist"]
        assert "com.duoface" in (root/"DEBIAN/control").read_text()
        ctrl=root/"DEBIAN/control"
        raw=ctrl.read_text()
        assert raw.count("Version: 0.1.0~native20")==1
        ctrl.write_text(raw.replace("Version: 0.1.0~native20","Version: "+VERSION,1))
        # Preserve exact migration installer bytes; settings owner stays DuoFace.
        orig_preinst=(root/"DEBIAN/preinst").read_bytes()
        run("sh","-n",root/"DEBIAN/preinst")
        assert b'cp -p "$old_store" "$new_store"' in orig_preinst
        (root/"DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("\n".join(
           hashlib.md5((root/rel).read_bytes()).hexdigest()+"  "+rel
           for rel in sorted(after))+"\n")
        a.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        run("dpkg-deb","--build","--root-owner-group",root,a.out)
        for k,expected in (("Package","com.duoface.independent"),("Version",VERSION),
                           ("Architecture","iphoneos-arm64")):
            assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(a.out),k],text=True).strip()==expected
        report={
           "status":"NATIVE21_NATIVE12_AUTOSTART_RESTORED_OBSOLETE_APP_TILE_REMOVED_NOT_DEVICE_TESTED",
           "version":VERSION,"package":"com.duoface.independent",
           "base_native20_sha256":PIN,
           "deb_sha256":sha(a.out.read_bytes()),
           "deb_size":a.out.stat().st_size,
           "original_payload_files":len(before),
           "unchanged_payload_files":len(before)-len(changed),
           "modified_payload_files":changed,
           "original_iphone_app_and_dashboard_binary_byte_identical":True,
           "previously_installed_apps_prefs_migration_unchanged":True,
           "process_filter_plists_byte_identical":True,
           "no_new_runtime_autostart_override":True,
           "restored_native12_method_owners":["CBBridgeManager.effectiveAutoLaunchBundleID",
                                              "CBBridgeManager.performAutoResume"],
           "native12_app_selection_on_iphone_preserved":True,
           "new_disconnection_grace_seconds":10,
           "source_contracts":gates,
           "full_layout_runtime_restoration_confirmed":False,
           "obsolete_button_device_tested":False,
           "license_modified":False,"dopamine_touched":False,
           "sileo_published":False,
           "notes":"Only 2 modules recompiled: revert extra single-app Auto Resume overrides while maintaining 10s close. Hide obsolete CarPlay Add App tile; iPhone native picker unchanged. Build/byte regressions cannot prove native UI restored on device."
        }
    a.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    a.report.write_text(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+"\n")
    print(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2))
if __name__=="__main__":main()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native21_native12_autoresume.py ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native3.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""DuoFace Native3: safe, fixed-length brand migration from working native2.

Rebuild from exactly pinned native2 DEB. Only translate legacy UI names in two
localization string pools, a single FullscreenBridge diagnostic and installer
collision guard. Keep native UI, picker, CarPlay split and add-on native2 as-is.

Every altered Mach-O is re-signed while preserving original entitlements.
An EXHAUSTIVE final DEB scan must find ZERO DuoNavi/Airaw bytes, paths or scripts.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
from collections import Counter
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import plistlib
import re
import stat
import subprocess
import tempfile

from audit_branding import hits, inspect_tar
import io
import tarfile

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
SRC=ROOT/"dist/payload-migration/native2/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native2_iphoneos-arm64.deb"
SRC_SHA="0d48d13e4c5965f8d787d86fa4c7776145cd843e98e7cae0b516779271264e60"
VERSION="0.1.0~native3"
PREFIX="var/jb"
UI=f"{PREFIX}/Applications/DuoFaceUI.app/DuoFaceUI"
DASH=f"{PREFIX}/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceDashboard.dylib"
FULL=f"{PREFIX}/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceFullscreenBridge.dylib"
NEW_BRANDING_FILES={UI,DASH,FULL}
NATIVE2_ROLLBACK="0d48d13e4c5965f8d787d86fa4c7776145cd843e98e7cae0b516779271264e60"
CONTROL_OVERWRITES={"control","md5sums","preinst"}

def sha256(data:bytes)->str:return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
def run(*parts): subprocess.run(list(parts),check=True)
def all_files(root:Path):
    return {p.relative_to(root).as_posix():sha256(p.read_bytes())
            for p in root.rglob("*") if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink()}
def replace_brand_strings(data:bytes):
    """All matching translated brand strings have exactly seven letters.

    Existing diagnostic report proves true occurrences are UTF-16LE localized
    Unicode strings (UTF-16BE offsets overlap by -1). We only patch UTF-16LE
    and reject any unexpected leftover ASCII/BE occurrence.
    """
    chars=[]
    for ch in "duonavi":
        choices=(ch.lower()+ch.upper()).encode("ascii")
        chars.append(b"["+choices+b"]\\x00")
    pattern=re.compile(b"".join(chars))
    matches=[m.group(0) for m in pattern.finditer(data)]
    def repl(match:re.Match[bytes]):
        name=match.group(0).decode("utf-16-le")
        changed="DUOFACE" if name.isupper() else "duoface" if name.islower() else "DuoFace"
        patched=changed.encode("utf-16-le")
        assert len(patched)==len(match.group(0)), "Refuse patch when Mach-O string byte length changes"
        return patched
    patched,count=pattern.subn(repl,data)
    assert count==len(matches)
    assert len(patched)==len(data), "Binary offsets moved"
    return patched,count

def sign_binary(path:Path,entitlements_path:Path):
    # Read existing entitlements via ldid before signing; preserve if nonempty.
    old=subprocess.run(["ldid","-e",str(path)],capture_output=True)
    if old.returncode==0 and b"<plist" in old.stdout:
        entitlements_path.write_bytes(old.stdout)
        run("ldid", "-S"+str(entitlements_path),str(path))
        return "preserved"
    run("ldid","-S",str(path))
    return "none-found"

def prepare_new_preinst(root:Path):
    # There are ZERO hard-coded legacy package IDs anywhere in this script.
    # Instead, guard *file ownership* so a rename never deletes an installed
    # package, even if its old package ID is unknown.
    content="""#!/bin/sh
set -eu
for protected in \
    /var/jb/Applications/DuoFaceUI.app/DuoFaceUI \
    /var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceDashboard.dylib \
    /var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceFullscreenBridge.dylib \
    /var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceVisualHook.dylib
do
    owner="$(dpkg-query -S "$protected" 2>/dev/null || true)"
    if [ -n "$owner" ]; then
        case "$owner" in
            "com.duoface.independent":* ) ;;
            *)
                echo "DuoFace native3: another installed package owns $protected; refusing overwrite" >&2
                exit 1
                ;;
        esac
    fi
done
exit 0
"""
    # The case code is intentionally simple to audit and cannot remove anything.
    out=root/"DEBIAN/preinst"
    out.write_text(content,encoding="utf-8")
    out.chmod(0o755)

def verify_local_payload(root:Path):
    # Path and link check independently of raw byte scan.
    for p in root.rglob("*"):
        assert not hits(p.relative_to(root).as_posix().encode("utf-8")), f"Legacy name in path: {p}"
        if p.is_symlink():assert not hits(os.readlink(p).encode("utf-8")), f"Legacy symlink: {p}"
        if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink():
            occurrences=hits(p.read_bytes())
            if occurrences:
                raise AssertionError(f"Legacy terms remain after branding migration: {p}: {occurrences[:2]}")

def main():
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--out",type=Path,required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--report",type=Path,required=True)
    args=ap.parse_args()
    source_bytes=SRC.read_bytes()
    assert sha256(source_bytes)==SRC_SHA, "Refuse if native2 baseline differs from pinned native2 release"
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-native3-") as work:
        directory=Path(work)
        root=directory/"staging"
        run("dpkg-deb","-R",str(SRC),str(root))
        original=all_files(root)
        baseline_control=(root/"DEBIAN/control").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        assert "Package: com.duoface.independent" in baseline_control
        assert "Version: 0.1.0~native2" in baseline_control
        original_macho={}
        brand_counts={}
        signatures={}
        for index,rel in enumerate((UI,DASH,FULL)):
            path=root/rel
            data=path.read_bytes()
            original_macho[rel]={"size":len(data),"sha256":sha256(data)}
            if rel in (UI,DASH):
                patched,count=replace_brand_strings(data)
                expected=434 if rel==UI else 428
                assert count==expected, f"Unexpected original translated string count for {rel}: {count}"
                brand_counts[rel]=count
            else:
                # One debug-only string. Both phrases have IDENTICAL byte width:
                # Airaw-compatible (16) -> DuoFace-friendly (16).
                old=b"Airaw-compatible"
                new=b"DuoFace-friendly"
                assert len(old)==len(new), "May not shift Mach-O string offsets"
                assert data.count(old)==1 and data.count(b"Airaw")==1, "Unexpected diagnostic context"
                patched=data.replace(old,new)
                brand_counts[rel]=1
            assert len(patched)==len(data)
            path.write_bytes(patched)
            # ldid resigns modified executable; retain original entitlements
            # rather than silently discarding CarPlay/application capabilities.
            # Reload previous entitlements from saved untouched source file.
            before=directory/f"before-{index}.bin"
            before.write_bytes(data)
            existing=subprocess.run(["ldid","-e",str(before)],capture_output=True)
            entitlement=directory/f"entitlements-{index}.xml"
            if existing.returncode==0 and b"<plist" in existing.stdout:
                entitlement.write_bytes(existing.stdout)
                run("ldid","-S"+str(entitlement),str(path))
                signatures[rel]="preserved_existing_entitlements"
            else:
                run("ldid","-S",str(path))
                signatures[rel]="ad_hoc_no_existing_entitlements"
            assert path.stat().st_size>10000
            assert not hits(path.read_bytes()), f"Legacy vendor string after signing: {rel}"
        # Replace the only original installer reference with a package-agnostic
        # ownership guard (safer than deleting collision protection).
        prepare_new_preinst(root)
        control=root/"DEBIAN/control"
        revised=baseline_control.replace("Version: 0.1.0~native2","Version: "+VERSION)
        assert revised!=baseline_control and revised.count("Version: "+VERSION)==1
        control.write_text(revised,encoding="utf-8")
        file_checksums=[]
        for p in sorted(root.rglob("*")):
            if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink() and p.relative_to(root).parts[0]!="DEBIAN":
                relative=p.relative_to(root).as_posix()
                file_checksums.append(f"{hashlib.md5(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()}  {relative}")
        (root/"DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("\n".join(file_checksums)+"\n",encoding="utf-8")
        (root/"DEBIAN/md5sums").chmod(0o644)
        now=all_files(root)
        changed={p for p in now if now[p]!=original.get(p)}
        assert changed==NEW_BRANDING_FILES | {f"DEBIAN/{x}" for x in CONTROL_OVERWRITES}, sorted(changed)
        assert set(now)==set(original), "Native3 must not add/remove any original app or library files"
        for rel in set(now)-changed:
            assert now[rel]==original[rel],f"Unexpected modified payload: {rel}"
        # Check all entries *including* installer scripts and executable bytes.
        verify_local_payload(root)
        args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        run("dpkg-deb","--build","--root-owner-group",str(root),str(args.out))
    report={
        "status":"NATIVE3_BRAND_CLEAN_DEB_BUILT_PENDING_DEVICE_CARPLAY_TEST",
        "version":VERSION,"package":"com.duoface.independent",
        "source_native2_sha256":SRC_SHA,
        "source_native2_preserved_for_rollback":True,
        "binary_localized_replacements":brand_counts,
        "re_signed_changed_binary_entitlements":signatures,
        "original_macho_before":original_macho,
        "modified_paths":sorted(changed),
        "unmodified_payload_path_count":len(now)-len(changed),
        "all_binary_sizes_constant_before_resigning":True,
        "zero_brand_matches_in_all_extracted_files":True,
        "no_auto_remove_legacy_packages":True,
        "native2_settings_cleanup_preserved":True,
        "license_logic_code_fully_removed":False,
        "carplay_device_tested":False,
        "sileo_published":False,
        "sha256":sha256(args.out.read_bytes()),
        "size":args.out.stat().st_size,
        "note":"Brand names are removed from static bytes and installer text, but remaining license code must be assessed independently.",
    }
    args.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    args.report.write_text(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2,sort_keys=True)+"\n",encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in report.items() if k not in ("original_macho_before",)},ensure_ascii=False,indent=2))
if __name__=="__main__":main()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native3.py ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native4.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Construct an experimental DuoFace native4 from byte-pinned native3.

Remove inactive subscription/payment/expiry implementation bodies while
preserving runtime selectors and Objective-C ABI to avoid stale call crashes.
The original CarPlay split manager, app picker, geometry and assets are kept.
Only ARM64 is packaged: this avoids mismatched arm64e metadata/fixup patches.
Report actual changed method bytes, never call this full-source recompilation.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import struct
import subprocess
import tempfile

from audit_native4_license_code import inspect, slurp_slices
from audit_branding import hits

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
SOURCE=ROOT/"dist/payload-migration/native3/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native3_iphoneos-arm64.deb"
EXPECTED="7040338f37ebb1d1d2bee5adffd9cc0558b595b1ebcb9283169ab8d7f71bf2df"
VERSION="0.1.0~native4"
UI="var/jb/Applications/DuoFaceUI.app/DuoFaceUI"
DASH="var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceDashboard.dylib"
PRESERVE_MANAGER={
    "init","sharedInstance","dealloc",".cxx_destruct",
    "isCarPlayScreenConnected",
    "isCarPlayScreen:","isCarPlayScreenConnectedExcludingScreen:",
}
PRESERVE_UI_LIFECYCLE={"dealloc",".cxx_destruct"}
SPECIFIC_UI={
    ("XXRootViewController","checkAndPresentLicenseModalIfNeeded"),
    ("CBGeneralSettingsViewController","openSubscriptionScreen"),
}
BRIDGE_METHODS={
    "presentLicenseWarningModalWithType:","dismissLicenseWarningModal",
    "startLicenseWatchdogTimer","stopLicenseWatchdogTimer",
    "licenseWatchdogTimerFired:","licenseWarningModal","setLicenseWarningModal:",
}
NOP=struct.pack("<I",0xd503201f)
RET=struct.pack("<I",0xd65f03c0)
MOV_X0_ZERO=struct.pack("<I",0xd2800000)
MOV_W0_ZERO=struct.pack("<I",0x52800000)
MOV_W0_ONE=struct.pack("<I",0x52800020)
MOV_X0_ONE=struct.pack("<I",0xd2800020)
# 0xffffffff as a stable, time-independent uptime compatibility scalar:
MOVZ_X0_FFFF=struct.pack("<I",0xd29fffe0)
MOVK_X0_FFFF_LSL16=struct.pack("<I",0xf2bfffe0)

def sha(b:bytes):return hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest()
def run(*args):subprocess.run(list(map(str,args)),check=True)
def eligible(name,selector):
    if (name,selector) in SPECIFIC_UI:return True
    if name.startswith("CBSubscription") or name=="CBLicenseExpiredModalViewController":
        return selector not in PRESERVE_UI_LIFECYCLE
    if name in ("CBLicenseShieldIconView","CBLicenseWarningModalView"):
        return selector not in PRESERVE_UI_LIFECYCLE
    if name=="CBLicenseSyncManager":
        return selector not in PRESERVE_MANAGER
    if name=="CBBridgeManager":return selector in BRIDGE_METHODS
    return False

def original_abi_stub(method):
    ret_type=method["return_type"]
    selector=method["selector"]
    if ret_type=="v":return RET
    if ret_type=="B" or ret_type=="c":
        return (MOV_W0_ONE if selector in ("isLicenseCurrentlyValid","installAndVerifyLicensePayload:errorMsg:") else MOV_W0_ZERO)+RET
    if ret_type in ("Q","q","I","i","l","L","S","s"):
        if selector in ("remainingLicenseSeconds","actualLicenseExpirationTimestamp"):
            return MOVZ_X0_FFFF+MOVK_X0_FFFF_LSL16+RET
        return MOV_X0_ZERO+RET
    if ret_type=="@":
        if selector.startswith("init") or selector=="sharedInstance":return RET
        return MOV_X0_ZERO+RET
    raise ValueError(f"Unhandled original ABI {ret_type!r} {method['class']}.{selector}")

def rewrite_arm64(original:bytes,name:str):
    slices=[x for x in slurp_slices(original) if x["arch"]=="arm64:0"]
    assert len(slices)==1,f"Missing generic arm64 architecture: {name}"
    raw=bytearray(slices[0]["bytes"])
    metadata=inspect(bytes(raw))
    items=metadata["license_named_methods"]
    assert items and not metadata["methods_without_safe_text_start"]
    selected=[]
    # Never overwrite shared host functions: each method starts at LC_FUNCTION_STARTS
    # and ends at the next recorded function start. Remove only exact methods.
    for m in items:
        if eligible(m["class"],m["selector"]):
            if not m["listed_in_lc_function_starts"] or not m["inside_executable_text"]:
                raise ValueError("Function start not established "+m["class"]+"."+m["selector"])
            off=int(m["imp_file_offset"],16)
            span=m["estimated_function_span"]
            stub=original_abi_stub(m)
            if not isinstance(span,int) or span< len(stub) or span%4!=0:
                raise ValueError("Invalid instruction span "+str(m))
            assert off%4==0
            selected.append((off,span,stub,m))
    if name=="DuoFaceUI":assert len(selected)>170,(name,len(selected))
    else:assert len(selected)>=35,(name,len(selected))
    selected.sort(key=lambda x:x[0])
    spans=[]
    for i,(off,size,stub,method) in enumerate(selected):
        if i>0 and off<selected[i-1][0]+selected[i-1][1]:
            raise ValueError("Function spans overlap: "+method["class"]+"."+method["selector"])
        assert off+size<=len(raw)
        if len(raw[off:off+size])!=size:raise ValueError("Truncated method")
        before=sha(raw[off:off+size])
        raw[off:off+size]=stub+NOP*((size-len(stub))//4)
        spans.append({
            "class":method["class"],"selector":method["selector"],
            "original_type":method["type_encoding"],
            "file_offset":off,"bytes_replaced":size,
            "previous_span_sha256":before,"stub_bytes":len(stub),
            "remaining_original_instruction_bytes_inside_span":0,
        })
    assert all(not hits(bytes(raw)) for _ in [0]),"Old vendor name reintroduced"
    return bytes(raw),spans,metadata

def patch(root:Path,work:Path):
    report={}
    for name,rel in (("DuoFaceUI",UI),("DuoFaceDashboard",DASH)):
        file=root/rel
        before=file.read_bytes()
        slim,methods,metadata=rewrite_arm64(before,name)
        # Narrow universal binaries to one tested generic ARM64 slice. Both
        # arm64/arm64e devices can load arm64 binaries; device testing required.
        original=work/(name+".before")
        original.write_bytes(before)
        build=work/(name+".thin")
        run("lipo","-thin","arm64","-output",build,original)
        assert sha(build.read_bytes())==sha(next(x["bytes"] for x in slurp_slices(before) if x["arch"]=="arm64:0"))
        file.write_bytes(slim)
        run("ldid","-S",file)
        post=subprocess.check_output(["lipo","-archs",str(file)],text=True).strip()
        assert post=="arm64",(name,post)
        newbytes=file.read_bytes()
        # Re-signing can adjust Mach-O load commands, but must not restore any
        # of the deactivated method instruction bodies.
        active=inspect(newbytes)
        assert active["method_count_license_related"]==metadata["method_count_license_related"]
        report[name]={
            "original_sha256":sha(before),"patched_sha256":sha(newbytes),
            "original_arches":[sl["arch"] for sl in slurp_slices(before)],
            "packaged_arches":["arm64"],
            "method_body_count":len(methods),
            "method_bytes_overwritten":sum(m["bytes_replaced"] for m in methods),
            "patched_methods":methods,
            "selector_tables_left_for_compatibility":True,
        }
    return report

def main():
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--out",type=Path,required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--report",type=Path,required=True)
    args=ap.parse_args()
    assert SOURCE.is_file()
    assert sha(SOURCE.read_bytes())==EXPECTED,"Never rebuild from an unknown DEB"
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-native4-") as tmp:
        root=Path(tmp)/"stage"
        run("dpkg-deb","-R",SOURCE,root)
        before={p.relative_to(root).as_posix():sha(p.read_bytes())
                for p in root.rglob("*") if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink()}
        patched=patch(root,Path(tmp))
        control=root/"DEBIAN/control"
        data=control.read_text()
        assert "Version: 0.1.0~native3" in data
        control.write_text(data.replace("Version: 0.1.0~native3","Version: "+VERSION))
        md5sums=[]
        for p in sorted(root.rglob("*")):
            if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink() and p.relative_to(root).parts[0]!="DEBIAN":
                md5sums.append(hashlib.md5(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()+"  "+p.relative_to(root).as_posix())
        (root/"DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("\n".join(md5sums)+"\n")
        changed={p.relative_to(root).as_posix() for p in root.rglob("*")
                 if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink() and sha(p.read_bytes())!=before.get(p.relative_to(root).as_posix())}
        assert changed=={UI,DASH,"DEBIAN/control","DEBIAN/md5sums"},changed
        for p in root.rglob("*"):
            if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink():
                assert not hits(p.read_bytes()),"Vendor names found in resulting file "+str(p)
        args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        run("dpkg-deb","--build","--root-owner-group",root,args.out)
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.out),"Package"],text=True).strip()=="com.duoface.independent"
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.out),"Version"],text=True).strip()==VERSION
    report={
        "status":"NATIVE4_EXPERIMENTAL_CODE_BODIES_REPLACED_NOT_CARPLAY_TESTED",
        "package":"com.duoface.independent","version":VERSION,
        "baseline_native3_sha256":EXPECTED,
        "method_bodies":patched,
        "changed_payload_paths":sorted(changed),
        "untouched_original_files":len(before)-len(changed),
        "compiled_original_carplay_picker_source_rewritten":False,
        "all_embedded_license_strings_removed":False,
        "all_embedded_license_code_proven_removed":False,
        "what_removed":"Selected original method implementation bodies replaced by ABI-compatible inert return stubs; selector metadata retained.",
        "no_remote_license_sync_in_replaced_methods":True,
        "other_unknown_code_paths_may_still_exist":True,
        "source_native3_preserved_for_rollback":True,
        "device_tested":False,"sileo_published":False,
        "deb_sha256":sha(args.out.read_bytes()),"deb_size":args.out.stat().st_size,
    }
    args.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    args.report.write_text(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+"\n",encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps({
        "version":VERSION,"status":report["status"],
        "methods_removed":{k:v["method_body_count"] for k,v in patched.items()},
        "bytes_replaced":{k:v["method_bytes_overwritten"] for k,v in patched.items()},
        "deb_sha256":report["deb_sha256"],"deb_size":report["deb_size"],
    },ensure_ascii=False,indent=2))
if __name__=="__main__":main()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native4.py ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native5.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""DuoFace native5: reversible final expiry UI quarantine over device-OK native4.

Treat this as a STAGED TRIAL, never as proof that all license code is gone:
* neutralize two late-expiry state methods in iPhone app with exact ARM64 ABI;
* mask only user-facing static license/activation wording, not selector/keys;
* generate a complete residual method list with "safe-next-action" categories;
* leave CBBridgeManager and all CarPlay lifecycle/host routines untouched.

All non-whitelisted payloads must remain byte-identical to native4.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import struct
import subprocess
import tempfile
from audit_native4_license_code import inspect
from audit_branding import hits

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
BASE=ROOT/"dist/payload-migration/native4/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native4_iphoneos-arm64.deb"
EXPECTED="517cc6ccf84716e40b80116834cf1c874e9ae5027584dc94118cc8f6c4f7f244"
VERSION="0.1.0~native5"
FILES={
    "DuoFaceUI":"var/jb/Applications/DuoFaceUI.app/DuoFaceUI",
    "DuoFaceDashboard":"var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceDashboard.dylib",
}
FLAG_METHODS={
    ("XXRootViewController","isShowingExpiredModal"):("B16@0:8",struct.pack("<II",0x52800000,0xd65f03c0)),
    ("XXRootViewController","setIsShowingExpiredModal:"):("v20@0:8B16",struct.pack("<I",0xd65f03c0)),
}
NOP=struct.pack("<I",0xd503201f)

# Static, human-visible text ONLY. No Objective-C class, selector, localization
# KEY_..., callback, API URL, resource path, ID or receipt data is edited.
PRESENTATION_ONLY=[
    "License Expired",
    "License Not Activated",
    "License Status",
    "DuoFace Pro License",
    "SUBSCRIPTION & LICENSE",
    "ENTER ACTIVATION CODE (GIFTCODE)",
    "License Download Incomplete",
    "Activation Failed",
    "Lifetime license. All features are permanently available.",
    "DuoFace requires an active license to run on CarPlay. Please activate to get started.",
    "Your DuoFace CarPlay license has expired. Please renew or enter an activation code to continue using the app on your vehicle.",
    "Your license period has ended. Please park your vehicle safely and open DuoFace on your iPhone to renew.",
    "Please park your vehicle safely and open DuoFace on your iPhone to activate your license.",
]
KEYWORDS=("license","licence","subscription","expiration","expired","activation","trial","giftcode","payment","receipt","hardwareid","deviceid")

def sha(data:bytes)->str:return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
def cmd(*args):subprocess.run(list(map(str,args)),check=True)

def sections(blob:bytes):
    assert blob[:4]==bytes.fromhex("cffaedfe"),"native4 only supports generic arm64 slice"
    n=struct.unpack_from("<I",blob,16)[0]
    cursor=32
    for _ in range(n):
        command,length=struct.unpack_from("<II",blob,cursor)
        assert length>=8 and cursor+length<=len(blob)
        if command==0x19:
            amount=struct.unpack_from("<I",blob,cursor+64)[0]
            for i in range(amount):
                at=cursor+72+i*80
                assert at+80<=cursor+length
                sec=blob[at:at+16].split(b"\x00")[0].decode("ascii","replace")
                seg=blob[at+16:at+32].split(b"\x00")[0].decode("ascii","replace")
                size=struct.unpack_from("<Q",blob,at+40)[0]
                offset=struct.unpack_from("<I",blob,at+48)[0]
                if offset+size<=len(blob):
                    yield seg,sec,offset,size
        cursor+=length

def replace_expired_flags(blob:bytearray):
    methods=inspect(bytes(blob))["license_named_methods"]
    targets={}
    for method in methods:
        key=(method["class"],method["selector"])
        if key in FLAG_METHODS:
            assert key not in targets,("duplicate original method",key)
            typ,stub=FLAG_METHODS[key]
            assert method["type_encoding"]==typ,(key,method["type_encoding"])
            assert method["listed_in_lc_function_starts"],key
            assert method["inside_executable_text"],key
            offset=int(method["imp_file_offset"],16)
            length=method["estimated_function_span"]
            assert length%4==0 and length>=len(stub)
            old=bytes(blob[offset:offset+length])
            new=stub+NOP*((length-len(stub))//4)
            assert old!=new,(key,"already neutralized; refuse redundant release")
            blob[offset:offset+length]=new
            targets[key]={
               "class":key[0],"selector":key[1],"signature":typ,
               "file_offset":offset,"byte_count":length,
               "before_sha256":sha(old),"after_sha256":sha(new),
               "replacement":"ABI-compatible false getter/no-op setter; original selector metadata retained",
               "state":"temporarily_disabled_native5",
               "next_after_device_pass":"Candidate to remove unused expiry-state storage/calls after static caller audit; retain ABI if needed",
            }
    assert len(targets)==2,("Expected both expired state methods",list(targets))
    return list(targets.values())

def mask_static_ui(blob:bytearray):
    changes=[]
    for seg,sec,off,length in sections(blob):
        if (seg,sec) not in {("__TEXT","__cstring"),("__TEXT","__ustring"),("__TEXT_EXEC","__cstring")}:continue
        bound=off+length
        enc="utf-16-le" if sec=="__ustring" else "utf-8"
        null=b"\x00\x00" if enc=="utf-16-le" else b"\x00"
        for label in PRESENTATION_ONLY:
            old=label.encode(enc)
            # Mask with spaces; keeps all pointers, file offsets, encodings and
            # original terminators. This *hides*, not deletes from source.
            new=" "*len(label)
            replacement=new.encode(enc)
            assert len(replacement)==len(old)
            cur=off
            while True:
                idx=bytes(blob).find(old,cur,bound)
                if idx<0:break
                end=idx+len(old)
                # Match full NUL-terminated literal, never a suffix of a key,
                # Objective-C method or class name.
                prev_ok=(idx==off or blob[idx-len(null):idx]==null)
                next_ok=(end+len(null)<=bound and blob[end:end+len(null)]==null)
                if prev_ok and next_ok:
                    blob[idx:end]=replacement
                    changes.append({"file_offset":idx,"section":seg+","+sec,
                                    "label":label,"encoding":enc,"byte_count":len(old),
                                    "before_sha256":sha(old),"after_sha256":sha(replacement),
                                    "state":"temporarily_hidden_native5",
                                    "next_after_device_pass":"Candidate for physical deletion only after verifying no referenced localization/data keys"})
                cur=end
    return changes

def categorise_remaining(binary_name,metadata,hidden):
    records=[]
    targets={(item["class"],item["selector"]) for item in hidden}
    for method in metadata["license_named_methods"]:
        key=(method["class"],method["selector"])
        if key in targets:
            state="temporarily_disabled_native5"
            next_step="Check callers; decide whether to remove storage and selector after native5 device PASS"
        elif method["class"]=="CBLicenseSyncManager" and (
             method["selector"] in {"isCarPlayScreenConnected","isCarPlayScreen:",
                                    "isCarPlayScreenConnectedExcludingScreen:"}):
            state="retain_carplay_screen_detection"
            next_step="Keep until code ownership refactoring; active CarPlay screen tracking"
        elif method["selector"] in {"dealloc",".cxx_destruct","init","sharedInstance"}:
            state="retain_runtime_lifecycle"
            next_step="Do not remove until object allocation/caller graph proves dead"
        else:
            state="retained_compatibility_or_unverified"
            next_step="Investigate callers and original binary reference paths before deletion"
        records.append({"binary":binary_name,"class":method["class"],"selector":method["selector"],
                        "kind":method["kind"],"method_type":method["type_encoding"],
                        "state":state,"next_step":next_step})
    return records

def preserve_entitlements_and_resign(file:Path,before:bytes,work:Path,key:str):
    old=work/(key+"-before.dylib")
    old.write_bytes(before)
    probe=subprocess.run(["ldid","-e",str(old)],capture_output=True)
    if probe.returncode==0 and b"<plist" in probe.stdout:
        ent=work/(key+"-entitlements.xml")
        ent.write_bytes(probe.stdout)
        cmd("ldid","-S"+str(ent),file)
        return "reused_prior_entitlements"
    cmd("ldid","-S",file)
    return "no_embedded_entitlements_found"

def main():
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--out",type=Path,required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--report",type=Path,required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--deletion-list",type=Path,required=True)
    args=parser.parse_args()
    assert sha(BASE.read_bytes())==EXPECTED,"Do not build from unknown native4 DEB"
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-native5-") as work:
        staging=Path(work)/"stage"
        cmd("dpkg-deb","-R",BASE,staging)
        original={p.relative_to(staging).as_posix():sha(p.read_bytes())
                  for p in staging.rglob("*") if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink()}
        payload={}
        todo=[]
        all_hidden=0
        for name,rel in FILES.items():
            file=staging/rel
            before=file.read_bytes()
            data=bytearray(before)
            hidden=replace_expired_flags(data) if name=="DuoFaceUI" else []
            # Scan remaining ObjC method signatures BEFORE masking data.
            all_methods=inspect(bytes(data))
            literal_masks=mask_static_ui(data)
            payload[name]={"method_flags":hidden,"masked_ui_literals":literal_masks,
                           "before_sha256":sha(before),"mask_count":len(literal_masks)}
            todo+=categorise_remaining(name,all_methods,hidden)
            all_hidden+=len(literal_masks)
            file.write_bytes(bytes(data))
            payload[name]["code_signing"]=preserve_entitlements_and_resign(file,before,Path(work),name)
            payload[name]["after_sha256"]=sha(file.read_bytes())
            assert len(file.read_bytes())>10000
            assert not hits(file.read_bytes())
        assert all_hidden>=4,("No meaningful static license UI strings found in native4",all_hidden)
        control=staging/"DEBIAN/control"
        text=control.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        assert "Package: com.duoface.independent" in text
        assert "Version: 0.1.0~native4" in text
        control.write_text(text.replace("Version: 0.1.0~native4","Version: "+VERSION),encoding="utf-8")
        md5=[]
        for p in sorted(staging.rglob("*")):
            if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink() and p.relative_to(staging).parts[0]!="DEBIAN":
                md5.append(hashlib.md5(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()+"  "+p.relative_to(staging).as_posix())
        (staging/"DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("\n".join(md5)+"\n")
        newer={p.relative_to(staging).as_posix():sha(p.read_bytes())
               for p in staging.rglob("*") if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink()}
        changed=sorted(x for x in newer if newer[x]!=original.get(x))
        expected_paths=sorted(list(FILES.values())+["DEBIAN/control","DEBIAN/md5sums"])
        assert changed==expected_paths,(changed,expected_paths)
        assert newer.keys()==original.keys(),"Packaging must not add/remove paths"
        for k in newer:
            if k not in changed:assert original[k]==newer[k],k
        args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        cmd("dpkg-deb","--build","--root-owner-group",staging,args.out)
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.out),"Version"],text=True).strip()==VERSION
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.out),"Package"],text=True).strip()=="com.duoface.independent"
    ledger={
        "status":"NATIVE5_RESIDUAL_LICENSE_DELETION_LEDGER_PENDING_REAL_DEVICE_APPROVAL",
        "baseline_native4_sha256":EXPECTED,
        "all_method_inventory":todo,
        "ui_literal_hidden_records":[{"binary":k,**m} for k,v in payload.items() for m in v["masked_ui_literals"]],
        "method_hidden_records":[{"binary":k,**m} for k,v in payload.items() for m in v["method_flags"]],
        "caution":"class names, Objective-C selector strings and lifecycle logic remain for ABI; hiding text or neutralizing a method is NOT proof of full removal",
        "next_after_carplay_pass":"Audit dynamic callers/initialization and physical delete only separately verified unused code/data.",
        "device_tested":False,
    }
    result={
       "status":"NATIVE5_STAGED_EXPIRED_UI_HIDDEN_NEEDS_DEVICE_TEST",
       "package":"com.duoface.independent","version":VERSION,
       "source_native4_sha256":EXPECTED,
       "changed_paths":changed,
       "untouched_files":len(original)-len(changed),
       "new_expired_state_methods_neutralized":sum(len(v["method_flags"]) for v in payload.values()),
       "hidden_literal_count":all_hidden,
       "changed_binaries":payload,
       "zero_old_brand_matches":True,
       "remaining_license_class_and_selector_metadata":True,
       "full_license_removal":False,
       "same_native_carplay_source_and_resources":True,
       "native4_rollback_file_preserved":True,
       "device_tested":False,"sileo_published":False,
       "deb_sha256":sha(args.out.read_bytes()),"deb_size":args.out.stat().st_size,
    }
    args.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    args.deletion_list.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    args.report.write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False)+"\n")
    args.deletion_list.write_text(json.dumps(ledger,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False)+"\n")
    print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in result.items() if k!="changed_binaries"},ensure_ascii=False,indent=2))
if __name__=="__main__":main()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native5.py ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native6.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""DuoFace Mới native6: erase ONLY 26 native5-hidden presentation literals.

This is targeted string-data excision, NOT full license-subsystem removal.
Do not remove ObjC class/selector metadata, lifecycle routines or CarPlay code.
The 2 expiry-modal method implementations were already inert in native5.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tempfile

from build_native5 import FILES, sections, preserve_entitlements_and_resign, sha, cmd
from audit_branding import hits

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
SOURCE = ROOT / "dist/payload-migration/native5/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native5_iphoneos-arm64.deb"
SOURCE_HASH = "9b0b45cb4f9f26ae7c16d9aea8366d032e337f262397e253e0078958d5a46a26"
LEDGER = ROOT / "dist/payload-migration/native5/DANH_SACH_CHO_XOA.json"
VERSION = "0.1.0~native6"


def erase_approved_display_strings(name: str, original: bytes, entries: list[dict]):
    data = bytearray(original)
    allowed_sections = {
        (seg + "," + sec, offset, offset + size)
        for seg, sec, offset, size in sections(original)
        if (seg, sec) in {("__TEXT", "__cstring"), ("__TEXT", "__ustring"),
                          ("__TEXT_EXEC", "__cstring")}
    }
    assert len(entries) == 13, (name, len(entries))
    used = set()
    removed = []
    for row in entries:
        start = int(row["file_offset"])
        count = int(row["byte_count"])
        end = start + count
        assert count > 0 and (start, end) not in used
        assert any(s == row["section"] and lo <= start < end <= hi
                   for s, lo, hi in allowed_sections), (name, row)
        used.add((start, end))
        old = bytes(data[start:end])
        # Native5 wrote only space bytes (UTF8 or UTF16); verify exact old SHA.
        assert sha(old) == row["after_sha256"], (name, row["label"])
        assert old == (" " * len(row["label"])).encode(row["encoding"])
        assert sha(row["label"].encode(row["encoding"])) == row["before_sha256"]
        # In native6 the old placeholder DATA BYTES are erased, not hidden
        # behind printable blanks. NUL termination remains safe for C pointers.
        new = bytes(count)
        data[start:end] = new
        removed.append({
            "binary": name, "file_offset": start, "byte_count": count,
            "original_label": row["label"], "section": row["section"],
            "encoding": row["encoding"], "native5_sha256": sha(old),
            "native6_sha256": sha(new), "operation": "zeroed_presentation_data"
        })
    intervals = sorted(used)
    assert all(a[1] <= b[0] for a, b in zip(intervals, intervals[1:]))
    return bytes(data), removed


def main():
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
    p.add_argument("--report", type=Path, required=True)
    args = p.parse_args()
    assert sha(SOURCE.read_bytes()) == SOURCE_HASH, "Unknown native5 baseline"
    ledger = json.loads(LEDGER.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    literals = ledger["ui_literal_hidden_records"]
    methods = ledger["method_hidden_records"]
    assert len(literals) == 26 and len(methods) == 2
    assert all(m["state"] == "temporarily_disabled_native5" for m in methods)
    assert {m["selector"] for m in methods} == {
        "isShowingExpiredModal", "setIsShowingExpiredModal:"}
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-native6-") as temp:
        tmp = Path(temp)
        stage = tmp / "stage"
        cmd("dpkg-deb", "-R", SOURCE, stage)
        original = {
            f.relative_to(stage).as_posix(): sha(f.read_bytes())
            for f in stage.rglob("*") if f.is_file() and not f.is_symlink()
        }
        operations = []
        signatures = {}
        for name, rel in FILES.items():
            f = stage / rel
            before = f.read_bytes()
            selected = [x for x in literals if x["binary"] == name]
            revised, erased = erase_approved_display_strings(name, before, selected)
            if name == "DuoFaceUI":
                for m in methods:
                    st = int(m["file_offset"])
                    end = st + int(m["byte_count"])
                    assert sha(before[st:end]) == m["after_sha256"]
                    assert before[st:end] == revised[st:end], "Must retain expiry ABI"
            assert len(revised) == len(before)
            f.write_bytes(revised)
            signatures[name] = preserve_entitlements_and_resign(f, before, tmp, name)
            assert not hits(f.read_bytes()), ("Old brand reintroduced", name)
            operations.extend(erased)
        assert len(operations) == 26
        control = stage / "DEBIAN/control"
        old_control = control.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        assert "Package: com.duoface.independent" in old_control
        assert "Version: 0.1.0~native5" in old_control
        control.write_text(old_control.replace(
            "Version: 0.1.0~native5", "Version: " + VERSION), encoding="utf-8")
        md5sums = []
        for f in sorted(stage.rglob("*")):
            if f.is_file() and not f.is_symlink() and f.relative_to(stage).parts[0] != "DEBIAN":
                md5sums.append(hashlib.md5(f.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
                               + "  " + f.relative_to(stage).as_posix())
        (stage / "DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("\n".join(md5sums) + "\n")
        current = {
            f.relative_to(stage).as_posix(): sha(f.read_bytes())
            for f in stage.rglob("*") if f.is_file() and not f.is_symlink()
        }
        assert original.keys() == current.keys()
        changed = sorted(k for k in original if original[k] != current[k])
        assert changed == sorted([
            "DEBIAN/control", "DEBIAN/md5sums", *FILES.values()
        ]), changed
        args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        cmd("dpkg-deb", "--build", "--root-owner-group", stage, args.out)
    assert subprocess.check_output(
        ["dpkg-deb", "-f", str(args.out), "Package"], text=True
    ).strip() == "com.duoface.independent"
    assert subprocess.check_output(
        ["dpkg-deb", "-f", str(args.out), "Version"], text=True
    ).strip() == VERSION
    report = {
        "status": "NATIVE6_ZERO_ERASED_DISPLAY_LITERALS_PENDING_CI_AND_DEVICE_TEST",
        "source_native5_sha256": SOURCE_HASH,
        "package": "com.duoface.independent", "version": VERSION,
        "zero_erased_presentation_literals": len(operations),
        "zero_erased_data_bytes": sum(x["byte_count"] for x in operations),
        "changes": operations, "changed_paths": changed,
        "untouched_files": len(original) - len(changed),
        "signatures": signatures,
        "expired_accessor_implementations": "Retained native5 inert ABI stubs",
        "license_class_selector_metadata_still_present": True,
        "all_embedded_license_code_proven_removed": False,
        "native5_and_native4_rollback_preserved": True,
        "carplay_executable_sections_modified": False,
        "device_tested": False, "sileo_published": False,
        "deb_sha256": sha(args.out.read_bytes()),
        "deb_size": args.out.stat().st_size
    }
    args.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    args.report.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n",
                           encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps({k: v for k, v in report.items() if k != "changes"},
                     indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native6.py ##########
