#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Native7 STAGED HIDE: user-visible license/subscription copy only.

39 explicitly audited NUL-terminated strings x2 binaries: replace with spaces
of identical byte count, retaining original terminators/pointers/selector/keys.
CarPlay hosts, runtime lifecycle, and every instruction remain untouched.
Native6 remains the immutable rollback; native7 is NOT physical deletion.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse, hashlib, json, subprocess, tempfile
from pathlib import Path
from build_native5 import FILES, sections, preserve_entitlements_and_resign, sha, cmd
from audit_branding import hits

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
SOURCE=ROOT/"dist/payload-migration/native6/com.duoface.independent_0.1.0~native6_iphoneos-arm64.deb"
EXPECTED="88b0e9358c075e2131ddb9e6ac25689584765d1285120e2e3282ce8a5bb3df15"
AUDIT=ROOT/"audit/payload-migration/NATIVE6_REMAINING_LICENSE_READONLY.json"
VERSION="0.1.0~native7"
# Only whole, visually presentable labels; all data keys, log tags, class names,
# ObjC selectors, synchronization identifiers and CarPlay control copy excluded.
UI_COPY=[
    "Connect to CarPlay to start your %ld-day free trial, or choose a plan below to activate.",
    "Connect to CarPlay for a free trial, or choose a plan below to activate.",
    "OR REDEEM GIFTCODE",
    "%ld-day trial ($0)",
    "Enter giftcode...",
    "Please enter a giftcode before activating.",
    "Giftcode is invalid or already used.",
    "Activated Successfully!",
    "DuoFace Pro license activated. All CarPlay multitasking features are ready to use!",
    "Awaiting Payment",
    "Order Expired",
    "Activate Now",
    "Lifetime license on this device.",
    "Connect to CarPlay to start your %ld-day free trial, or select a plan below.",
    "Connect to CarPlay to start your free trial, or select a plan below.",
    "Resume Pending Payment Page",
    "Enter activation code from your reseller or direct purchase:",
    "Don't have a code? Contact your reseller or support team for quick activation.",
    "Enter activation code...",
    "Reopen Payment Page",
    "Please enter an activation code before continuing.",
    "Activation code is invalid or already used.",
    "License Sync Incomplete",
    "DuoFace Pro license activated successfully. Connect to CarPlay to enjoy multitasking!",
    "Unable to load subscription plans. Please check your network connection and try again.",
    "Loading subscription plans...",
    "License Duration",
    "Licencia no activada",
    "Licencia caducada",
    "Guarda este ID de dispositivo para transferir tu licencia a otro equipo (hasta 10 veces).",
    "Estado de licencia",
    "Entrez le code d'activation de votre revendeur ou du support :",
    "Expiration serveur",
    "Statut de licence",
    "Voer activatiecode van verkoper of ondersteuning in:",
    "Licencja nieaktywna",
    "Licencja nieaktywowana",
    "Okres licencji",
    "Status licencji"
]


def mask_selected(name, before, catalog):
    targets=[x for x in catalog if x["display_literal"] in UI_COPY]
    assert len(targets)==len(UI_COPY)==39, (name,len(targets))
    assert {x["display_literal"] for x in targets}==set(UI_COPY)
    segmap={(seg,sec):(off,off+size) for seg,sec,off,size in sections(before)}
    data=bytearray(before)
    records=[]
    for row in targets:
        start=row["file_offset"]
        size=row["byte_count"]
        stop=start+size
        sec=row["section"]
        assert sec=="__cstring"
        assert any(key[1]==sec and lo<=start<stop<=hi for key,(lo,hi) in segmap.items())
        old=bytes(data[start:stop])
        assert old==row["display_literal"].encode("utf-8")
        assert sha(old)==row["sha256"]
        assert data[stop:stop+1]==b"\x00"
        assert data[start-1:start] in (b"\x00",b"") # whole C string
        replacement=b" "*size
        assert len(old)==len(replacement)
        data[start:stop]=replacement
        records.append({
            "binary":name,"label":row["display_literal"],"file_offset":start,
            "byte_count":size,"native6_sha256":sha(old),
            "native7_sha256":sha(replacement),"section":"__cstring",
            "state":"temporarily_hidden_native7",
        })
    pairs=sorted((x["file_offset"],x["file_offset"]+x["byte_count"]) for x in records)
    assert all(a[1]<=b[0] for a,b in zip(pairs,pairs[1:]))
    return bytes(data),records


def main():
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--out",type=Path,required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--report",type=Path,required=True)
    args=ap.parse_args()
    assert sha(SOURCE.read_bytes())==EXPECTED
    audit=json.loads(AUDIT.read_text())
    assert audit["native6_sha256"]==EXPECTED and audit["raw_deb_unchanged"]
    assert len(UI_COPY)==len(set(UI_COPY))==39
    records=[]
    signs={}
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-native7-") as temp:
        temp=Path(temp)
        stage=temp/"stage"
        cmd("dpkg-deb","-R",SOURCE,stage)
        prior={p.relative_to(stage).as_posix():sha(p.read_bytes())
               for p in stage.rglob("*") if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink()}
        for name,rel in FILES.items():
            p=stage/rel
            original=p.read_bytes()
            revised,changed=mask_selected(name,original,audit["binaries"][name]["strings"])
            assert len(revised)==len(original)
            p.write_bytes(revised)
            signs[name]=preserve_entitlements_and_resign(p,original,temp,name)
            assert not hits(p.read_bytes())
            records.extend(changed)
        assert len(records)==78
        control=stage/"DEBIAN/control"
        c=control.read_text()
        assert "Package: com.duoface.independent" in c
        assert "Version: 0.1.0~native6" in c
        control.write_text(c.replace("Version: 0.1.0~native6","Version: "+VERSION))
        md5sums=[]
        for p in sorted(stage.rglob("*")):
            if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink() and p.relative_to(stage).parts[0]!="DEBIAN":
                md5sums.append(hashlib.md5(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()+"  "+p.relative_to(stage).as_posix())
        (stage/"DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("\n".join(md5sums)+"\n")
        after={p.relative_to(stage).as_posix():sha(p.read_bytes())
               for p in stage.rglob("*") if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink()}
        changed_paths=sorted(k for k in prior if prior[k]!=after[k])
        assert prior.keys()==after.keys()
        assert changed_paths==sorted([*FILES.values(),"DEBIAN/control","DEBIAN/md5sums"])
        args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        cmd("dpkg-deb","--build","--root-owner-group",stage,args.out)
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.out),"Package"],text=True).strip()=="com.duoface.independent"
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.out),"Version"],text=True).strip()==VERSION
    report={
      "status":"NATIVE7_ONLY_STATIC_UI_COPY_STAGED_HIDE_NEEDS_DEVICE_TEST",
      "source_native6_sha256":EXPECTED,"package":"com.duoface.independent",
      "version":VERSION,"presentation_labels_masked":39,
      "presentation_literal_occurrences_masked":len(records),
      "presentation_bytes_masked":sum(x["byte_count"] for x in records),
      "masks":records,"changed_paths":changed_paths,
      "unchanged_all_other_payload_files":len(prior)-len(changed_paths),
      "machine_code_changed":False,"objc_metadata_or_keys_changed":False,
      "carplay_host_and_screen_detection_changed":False,
      "lifecycle_changed":False,"license_full_removal":False,
      "signatures":signs,"native6_rollback_preserved":True,
      "device_tested":False,"sileo_published":False,
      "deb_sha256":sha(args.out.read_bytes()),"deb_size":args.out.stat().st_size}
    args.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    args.report.write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False)+"\n")
    print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in report.items() if k!="masks"},indent=2,ensure_ascii=False))
if __name__=="__main__":main()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native7.py ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native8.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Native8: staged native7 39-label presentation hide + source-built 0.30s logo-grip hook.

Immutable source native6. EXACT two existing Mach-O binaries change in approved
__cstring literal ranges; all original code/CarPlay/ObjC metadata remains byte-identical.
The original DuoFaceVisualHook.dylib alone is replaced by source-compiled hook.
No other payload file or legacy package ID may change.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import subprocess
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path
from build_native5 import FILES, preserve_entitlements_and_resign, sha, cmd
from build_native7 import SOURCE, EXPECTED, AUDIT, UI_COPY, mask_selected
from audit_branding import hits

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
VERSION = "0.1.0~native8"
HOOK_REL = "var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceVisualHook.dylib"
HOOK_FILTER_REL = "var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceVisualHook.plist"


def files_and_digests(stage):
    return {
        p.relative_to(stage).as_posix(): sha(p.read_bytes())
        for p in stage.rglob("*") if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink()
    }


def build():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--hook", required=True, type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--out", required=True, type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--report", required=True, type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    assert sha(SOURCE.read_bytes()) == EXPECTED, "native6 baseline changed"
    catalog = json.loads(AUDIT.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert catalog["native6_sha256"] == EXPECTED and catalog["raw_deb_unchanged"]
    assert len(UI_COPY) == len(set(UI_COPY)) == 39
    assert args.hook.is_file() and args.hook.stat().st_size > 10000
    hook_source = ROOT / "src/DuoFaceVisualHook.m"
    grip_source = ROOT / "src/DFDividerLogoGrip.inc"
    hook_code = hook_source.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    grip_code = grip_source.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert '#include "DFDividerLogoGrip.inc"' in hook_code
    assert "DFInstallLogoGripHooks(hook, divider)" in hook_code
    assert "kDFLogoHoldThreshold = 0.30" in grip_code
    assert "DFLogoMoveWithHold" in grip_code
    assert "setSplitRatio:" not in grip_code
    assert subprocess.check_output(
        ["dpkg-deb", "-f", str(SOURCE), "Package"], text=True
    ).strip() == "com.duoface.independent"
    assert subprocess.check_output(
        ["dpkg-deb", "-f", str(SOURCE), "Version"], text=True
    ).strip() == "0.1.0~native6"

    records, signing = [], {}
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-native8-") as td:
        root = Path(td)
        stage = root / "stage"
        cmd("dpkg-deb", "-R", SOURCE, stage)
        original = files_and_digests(stage)
        assert HOOK_REL in original and HOOK_FILTER_REL in original
        assert all(rel in original for rel in FILES.values())

        for name, rel in FILES.items():
            target = stage / rel
            before = target.read_bytes()
            result, masks = mask_selected(name, before, catalog["binaries"][name]["strings"])
            assert len(result) == len(before), "Mach-O length changed"
            target.write_bytes(result)
            signing[name] = preserve_entitlements_and_resign(target, before, root, name)
            assert not hits(target.read_bytes()), "Old brand present"
            records.extend(masks)
        assert len(records) == 78
        assert all(m["state"] == "temporarily_hidden_native7" for m in records)

        original_hook = (stage / HOOK_REL).read_bytes()
        new_hook = args.hook.read_bytes()
        assert new_hook[:4] == bytes.fromhex("cffaedfe"), "Hook not ARM64 Mach-O"
        (stage / HOOK_REL).write_bytes(new_hook)
        signing["DuoFaceVisualHook"] = preserve_entitlements_and_resign(
            stage / HOOK_REL, original_hook, root, "DuoFaceVisualHook"
        )
        new_hook_signed_sha256 = sha((stage / HOOK_REL).read_bytes())
        assert new_hook_signed_sha256 != sha(original_hook)
        assert (stage / HOOK_FILTER_REL).is_file()
        assert not hits((stage / HOOK_REL).read_bytes())

        control = stage / "DEBIAN/control"
        old_control = control.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        assert "Package: com.duoface.independent" in old_control
        assert "Version: 0.1.0~native6" in old_control
        control.write_text(
            old_control.replace("Version: 0.1.0~native6", "Version: " + VERSION),
            encoding="utf-8",
        )
        md5sums = []
        for p in sorted(stage.rglob("*")):
            if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink() and p.relative_to(stage).parts[0] != "DEBIAN":
                md5sums.append(
                    hashlib.md5(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest() + "  " +
                    p.relative_to(stage).as_posix()
                )
        (stage / "DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("\n".join(md5sums) + "\n")
        updated = files_and_digests(stage)
        assert original.keys() == updated.keys(), "payload added or dropped files"
        changed = sorted(k for k in original if original[k] != updated[k])
        expected = sorted([
            "DEBIAN/control", "DEBIAN/md5sums", HOOK_REL, *FILES.values()
        ])
        assert changed == expected, (changed, expected)

        args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        assert not args.out.exists(), "Refusing to overwrite existing native8 DEB"
        cmd("dpkg-deb", "--build", "--root-owner-group", stage, args.out)

    assert subprocess.check_output(
        ["dpkg-deb", "-f", str(args.out), "Package"], text=True
    ).strip() == "com.duoface.independent"
    assert subprocess.check_output(
        ["dpkg-deb", "-f", str(args.out), "Version"], text=True
    ).strip() == VERSION
    assert subprocess.check_output(
        ["dpkg-deb", "-f", str(args.out), "Architecture"], text=True
    ).strip() == "iphoneos-arm64"
    result = {
        "status": "NATIVE8_TWO_SCOPE_STAGED_LICENSE_COPY_AND_LOGO_DRAG_NEEDS_DEVICE_QA",
        "baseline_native6_sha256": EXPECTED,
        "package": "com.duoface.independent",
        "version": VERSION,
        "target_rootless_architecture": "iphoneos-arm64",
        "license_ui_strings_selected": len(UI_COPY),
        "license_ui_literal_occurrences_hidden": len(records),
        "license_ui_selected_bytes": sum(x["byte_count"] for x in records),
        "actual_changed_literal_bytes_expected": sum(
            sum(v != 0x20 for v in m["label"].encode("utf-8")) for m in records
        ),
        "masks": records,
        "replaced_hook": HOOK_REL,
        "original_hook_sha256": sha(original_hook),
        "new_hook_sha256": new_hook_signed_sha256,
        "hook_compiled_input_sha256": sha(new_hook),
        "hook_source_sha256": sha(hook_source.read_bytes()),
        "grip_source_sha256": sha(grip_source.read_bytes()),
        "hold_duration_seconds": 0.30,
        "changed_payload_and_control_paths": changed,
        "unchanged_payload_files": len(original) - len(changed),
        "signing": signing,
        "carplay_host_and_detectors_modified": False,
        "native_host_code_modified": False,
        "objc_class_selector_metadata_modified": False,
        "license_full_removal_claimed": False,
        "device_tested": False,
        "sileo_published": False,
        "native6_rollback_preserved": True,
        "deb_sha256": sha(args.out.read_bytes()),
        "deb_size": args.out.stat().st_size,
    }
    args.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    args.report.write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in result.items() if k != "masks"}, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    build()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native8.py ##########

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native9.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Native9 CarPlay repair candidate: native6 ORIGINAL visuals + isolated logo.

This script intentionally does NOT rebuild/replace DuoFaceVisualHook.dylib.
Only the 39 x 2 already audited static license-display literals are masked,
and a separately compiled/signed DuoFaceLogoGripOnly.dylib + EXACT original
Substrate injection filter are added. Native divider pan is not hooked.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import plistlib
import subprocess
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path
from build_native5 import FILES, preserve_entitlements_and_resign, sha, cmd
from build_native7 import SOURCE, EXPECTED, AUDIT, UI_COPY, mask_selected
from build_native8 import files_and_digests
from audit_branding import hits

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
VERSION = "0.1.0~native9"
BASE_VISUAL = "var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceVisualHook.dylib"
BASE_FILTER = BASE_VISUAL.removesuffix(".dylib") + ".plist"
GRIP_DYLIB = "var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceLogoGripOnly.dylib"
GRIP_FILTER = GRIP_DYLIB.removesuffix(".dylib") + ".plist"
ORIGINAL_VISUAL_SHA256 = "4ebf68b630c102dee1bbaac87dcadddd3e13f618331cacd9c47ae1a27437959d"

def validate_source():
    standalone = (ROOT/"src/DuoFaceLogoGripOnly.m").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    gesture = (ROOT/"src/DFDividerLogoGrip.inc").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "#define DF_LOGO_PRESERVE_NATIVE_DIVIDER_PAN 1" in standalone
    assert '#include "DFDividerLogoGrip.inc"' in standalone
    assert "DFInstallLogoGripHooks(hook, divider)" in standalone
    assert "kDFLogoHoldThreshold = 0.30" in gesture
    assert "DFLogoMoveWithHold" in gesture
    assert "setSplitRatio:" not in gesture
    assert "DFSetButtonBackgroundColor" not in standalone
    assert "_expandButton" not in standalone and "_restoreButton" not in standalone
    assert "DFLogoDividerPan" not in standalone
    for marker in ("DFLogoGripHold", "DFLogoGripDown", "DFLogoGripUp",
                   "DFLogoRestorePositionBeforeLayout"):
        assert marker in gesture
    qa = json.loads((ROOT/"audit/native9/LOGO_ISOLATION_SOURCE_ONLY.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert qa["status"] == "SOURCE_ONLY_NATIVE9_LOGO_ISOLATION_ROUTE_PASS"
    assert all(qa["cases"].values()) and len(qa["cases"]) == 16
    return {
        "grip_only_source_sha256": sha((ROOT/"src/DuoFaceLogoGripOnly.m").read_bytes()),
        "gesture_source_sha256": sha((ROOT/"src/DFDividerLogoGrip.inc").read_bytes()),
        "source_only_qa_report_sha256": sha((ROOT/"audit/native9/LOGO_ISOLATION_SOURCE_ONLY.json").read_bytes()),
    }

def package():
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--hook", required=True, type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--out", required=True, type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--report", required=True, type=Path)
    args=parser.parse_args()
    sources=validate_source()
    assert sha(SOURCE.read_bytes()) == EXPECTED, "native6 baseline SHA changed"
    old_catalog=json.loads(AUDIT.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert old_catalog["native6_sha256"]==EXPECTED and old_catalog["raw_deb_unchanged"]
    assert len(UI_COPY)==len(set(UI_COPY))==39
    assert args.hook.is_file() and args.hook.stat().st_size>10000
    hook=args.hook.read_bytes()
    assert hook[:4]==bytes.fromhex("cffaedfe"), "not ARM64 Mach-O"
    assert b"DuoFaceGripOnly" in hook and b"DuoFaceGrip" in hook
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(SOURCE),"Package"],text=True).strip()=="com.duoface.independent"
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(SOURCE),"Version"],text=True).strip()=="0.1.0~native6"
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(SOURCE),"Architecture"],text=True).strip()=="iphoneos-arm64"

    args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    assert not args.out.exists(), "Refuse to overwrite existing native9 DEB"
    masks=[]
    signed={}
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-native9-") as td:
        temp=Path(td)
        stage=temp/"stage"
        cmd("dpkg-deb","-R",SOURCE,stage)
        before=files_and_digests(stage)
        assert BASE_VISUAL in before and BASE_FILTER in before
        assert GRIP_DYLIB not in before and GRIP_FILTER not in before
        for rel in FILES.values(): assert rel in before
        base_filter=(stage/BASE_FILTER).read_bytes()
        filter_plist=plistlib.loads(base_filter)
        # No made-up process list: the additive tweak inherits EXACT native6 filter.
        assert filter_plist.get("Filter") and filter_plist["Filter"].get("Bundles")
        assert sha((stage/BASE_VISUAL).read_bytes())==ORIGINAL_VISUAL_SHA256

        for name,rel in FILES.items():
            target=stage/rel
            src=target.read_bytes()
            masked,found=mask_selected(name,src,old_catalog["binaries"][name]["strings"])
            assert len(src)==len(masked)
            target.write_bytes(masked)
            signed[name]=preserve_entitlements_and_resign(target,src,temp,name)
            assert not hits(target.read_bytes())
            masks.extend(found)
        assert len(masks)==78
        assert all(x["state"]=="temporarily_hidden_native7" for x in masks)

        grip=stage/GRIP_DYLIB
        grip.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        grip.write_bytes(hook)
        # This independent MRC hook has no dedicated entitlement dependency.
        cmd("ldid","-S",grip)
        signed_grip_sha=sha(grip.read_bytes())
        (stage/GRIP_FILTER).write_bytes(base_filter)
        assert (stage/GRIP_FILTER).read_bytes()==(stage/BASE_FILTER).read_bytes()
        assert not hits(grip.read_bytes())

        control=stage/"DEBIAN/control"
        initial_control=control.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        assert "Package: com.duoface.independent" in initial_control
        assert "Version: 0.1.0~native6" in initial_control
        control.write_text(initial_control.replace("Version: 0.1.0~native6", "Version: "+VERSION),
                           encoding="utf-8")

        md5=[]
        for p in sorted(stage.rglob("*")):
            if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink() and p.relative_to(stage).parts[0]!="DEBIAN":
                md5.append(hashlib.md5(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()+"  "+p.relative_to(stage).as_posix())
        (stage/"DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("\n".join(md5)+"\n")
        after=files_and_digests(stage)
        assert set(after)-set(before)=={GRIP_DYLIB,GRIP_FILTER}
        assert not (set(before)-set(after)), "Existing payload cannot lose any files"
        changed=sorted(k for k in before if before[k]!=after[k])
        expected=sorted([*FILES.values(),"DEBIAN/control","DEBIAN/md5sums"])
        assert changed==expected,(changed,expected)
        assert before[BASE_VISUAL]==after[BASE_VISUAL]==ORIGINAL_VISUAL_SHA256
        assert before[BASE_FILTER]==after[BASE_FILTER]
        assert len(after)==len(before)+2
        assert sum(1 for x in masks if x["binary"]=="DuoFaceUI")==39
        assert sum(1 for x in masks if x["binary"]=="DuoFaceDashboard")==39
        cmd("dpkg-deb","--build","--root-owner-group",stage,args.out)

    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.out),"Package"],text=True).strip()=="com.duoface.independent"
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.out),"Version"],text=True).strip()==VERSION
    assert subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(args.out),"Architecture"],text=True).strip()=="iphoneos-arm64"
    result={
        "status":"NATIVE9_ORIGINAL_VISUAL_PLUS_GRIP_ONLY_STAGED_NEEDS_CARPLAY_DEVICE_QA",
        "native6_base_sha256":EXPECTED,
        "package":"com.duoface.independent",
        "version":VERSION,
        "architecture":"iphoneos-arm64",
        "license_labels_selected":39,
        "license_copy_occurrences_hidden":len(masks),
        "literal_selected_bytes":sum(m["byte_count"] for m in masks),
        "actual_changed_literal_bytes_expected":sum(
            sum(x!=0x20 for x in m["label"].encode("utf-8")) for m in masks),
        "masks":masks,
        "original_visual_sha256_unchanged":ORIGINAL_VISUAL_SHA256,
        "original_visual_filter_sha256_unchanged":before[BASE_FILTER],
        "new_grip_dylib":GRIP_DYLIB,
        "new_grip_sha256":signed_grip_sha,
        "new_grip_filter":GRIP_FILTER,
        "new_grip_filter_sha256":before[BASE_FILTER],
        "hook_input_sha256":sha(hook),
        "hook_src":sources,
        "changed_existing_paths":changed,
        "added_paths":sorted([GRIP_DYLIB,GRIP_FILTER]),
        "untouched_existing_payload_files":len(before)-len(changed),
        "resigned_existing":signed,
        "native_divider_pan_hooked":False,
        "fullscreen_legacy_visual_hook_replaced":False,
        "source_native6_rollback_preserved":True,
        "native8_rollback_preserved":True,
        "logo_hold_duration_seconds":0.30,
        "license_code_fully_removed":False,
        "device_tested":False,
        "sileo_published":False,
        "deb_sha256":sha(args.out.read_bytes()),
        "deb_bytes":args.out.stat().st_size,
    }
    args.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    args.report.write_text(json.dumps(result,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+"\n",encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in result.items() if k!="masks"},ensure_ascii=False,indent=2))

if __name__=="__main__":
    package()

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_visual12_duodash_trace.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Package a reversible DuoDash-focused activation trace on top of VisualTest12.

This is NOT an activation fix. No DuoDash/Airaw/proprietary binary is bundled.
The preserved VisualTest12 UI/Dashboard, all baseline assets, and separate Hub
are byte-identical. Only two diagnostic files and package metadata are added.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import plistlib
import subprocess
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
BASE = ROOT / ("dist/visualtest12-connectprobe1/"
               "com.duonavi.app_1.0.3-12~visualtest12launch2probe1_iphoneos-arm64.deb")
BASE_SHA256 = "43c7ffbaae23fa39a19c63e276f50cfd35c66ce1ea7ab887f9a78cc077738841"
PACKAGE = "com.duonavi.app"
OLD_VERSION = "1.0.3-12~visualtest12launch2probe1"
VERSION = "1.0.3-12~visualtest12launch3ddtrace1"
LIB_NAME = "DFDuoDashActivationTrace.dylib"
PLIST_NAME = "DFDuoDashActivationTrace.plist"
INJECT = "var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries"
FILTER = ROOT / "runtime/visualtest12/DFDuoDashActivationTrace.plist"
EXPECTED_BUNDLES = {
    "com.apple.springboard",
    "com.apple.CarPlayApp",
    "com.duoface.duofaceui",
    "com.duonavi.app",
}

def sha(blob: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(blob).hexdigest()

def field(package: Path, name: str) -> str:
    return subprocess.check_output(
        ["dpkg-deb", "-f", str(package), name], text=True
    ).strip()

def all_regular_files(root: Path) -> dict[str, str]:
    return {
        p.relative_to(root).as_posix(): sha(p.read_bytes())
        for p in root.rglob("*") if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink()
    }

def all_symlinks(root: Path) -> dict[str, str]:
    return {
        p.relative_to(root).as_posix(): str(p.readlink())
        for p in root.rglob("*") if p.is_symlink()
    }

def run(*args: object) -> None:
    subprocess.run([str(s) for s in args], check=True)

def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--dylib", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--report", type=Path, required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()

    assert BASE.is_file(), f"Missing exact baseline: {BASE}"
    assert sha(BASE.read_bytes()) == BASE_SHA256, "baseline DEB fingerprint changed"
    assert field(BASE, "Package") == PACKAGE
    assert field(BASE, "Version") == OLD_VERSION
    assert field(BASE, "Architecture") == "iphoneos-arm64"
    assert args.dylib.is_file() and args.dylib.stat().st_size > 4000
    dylib = args.dylib.read_bytes()
    assert dylib[:4] == bytes.fromhex("cffaedfe"), "expected signed ARM64 Mach-O"
    filters = plistlib.loads(FILTER.read_bytes())
    assert set(filters["Filter"]["Bundles"]) == EXPECTED_BUNDLES
    assert not args.out.exists(), "refuse accidental overwrite"
    assert args.out.resolve() != BASE.resolve()

    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="duoface-ddtrace-") as dirname:
        root = Path(dirname) / "payload"
        run("dpkg-deb", "-R", BASE, root)
        before = all_regular_files(root)
        before_links = all_symlinks(root)
        targets = {f"{INJECT}/{LIB_NAME}", f"{INJECT}/{PLIST_NAME}"}
        assert not (targets & set(before)), "diagnostic already in source package"

        output = root / INJECT
        output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (output / LIB_NAME).write_bytes(dylib)
        (output / PLIST_NAME).write_bytes(FILTER.read_bytes())

        control = root / "DEBIAN/control"
        text = control.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        oldline = f"Version: {OLD_VERSION}\n"
        assert text.count(oldline) == 1
        control.write_text(text.replace(oldline, f"Version: {VERSION}\n"),
                           encoding="utf-8")
        checksums = []
        for file in sorted(root.rglob("*")):
            if file.is_file() and not file.is_symlink():
                relative = file.relative_to(root).as_posix()
                if not relative.startswith("DEBIAN/"):
                    checksums.append(hashlib.md5(file.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
                                     + "  " + relative)
        (root / "DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("\n".join(checksums) + "\n")

        after = all_regular_files(root)
        assert all_symlinks(root) == before_links, "original symlinks changed"
        assert set(after) - set(before) == targets
        assert not set(before) - set(after), "source file disappeared"
        changed = sorted(k for k in before if before[k] != after[k])
        assert changed == ["DEBIAN/control", "DEBIAN/md5sums"], changed
        # Original payload and existing connection probe are untouched.
        original_payload = [k for k in before if k.startswith("var/jb/")]
        assert all(before[k] == after[k] for k in original_payload)

        args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        run("dpkg-deb", "--build", "--root-owner-group", root, args.out)

    assert field(args.out, "Package") == PACKAGE
    assert field(args.out, "Version") == VERSION
    assert field(args.out, "Architecture") == "iphoneos-arm64"
    report = {
        "status": "VISUALTEST12_DUODASH_ACTIVATION_TRACE_BUILD",
        "package": PACKAGE, "version": VERSION,
        "baseline_package_sha256": BASE_SHA256,
        "baseline_version": OLD_VERSION,
        "added_files": sorted(targets),
        "modified_files": changed,
        "removed_files": [],
        "original_visualtest12_core_unchanged": True,
        "existing_connection_probe_unchanged": True,
        "airaw_code_or_binary_bundled": False,
        "duodash_binary_bundled": False,
        "duodash_process_events_targeted": [
            "CBBridgeManagerDashboard.requestActivation:",
            "CBBridgeManagerDashboard.startBridging:",
            "CBBridgeManagerDashboard.prepareAndRequestActivation:",
            "CBBridgeManagerDashboard.bridgeDidActivate:",
            "CBBridgeManager.requestStateChange:",
            "CBBridgeManager.loadNewAppFromCurrentRequest:",
            "CBBridgeManager.handleCarPlayConnectionChanged:",
        ],
        "separate_hub_modified": False,
        "activation_fixed": False,
        "app_bridge_fixed": False,
        "device_tested": False,
        "sileo_published": False,
        "log_location": "/var/mobile/Documents/DuoFaceDuoDashTrace/{SpringBoard,CarPlayApp,DuoFaceUI}.jsonl",
        "new_dylib_sha256": sha(dylib),
        "new_filter_sha256": sha(FILTER.read_bytes()),
        "output_deb_sha256": sha(args.out.read_bytes()),
        "output_deb_bytes": args.out.stat().st_size,
    }
    args.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    args.report.write_text(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n",
                           encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_visual12_duodash_trace.py ##########

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Build a FUNCTIONAL three-getter VisualTest12 repair, not another log probe.

Source must be exact original VisualTest12, NOT mass-neutralized launchfix1 or
any independent Hub binary. Reversible candidate; real CarPlay QA pending.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import plistlib
import subprocess
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
SOURCE=ROOT/"dist/visual-1.0.3-12/com.duoface.app_1.0.3-12_visualtest_iphoneos-arm64.deb"
PIN="8b7dedf77b9ec05465b83fdb6e2d7127800eac232a34ecf12f2c08b3983a481a"
OLD="1.0.3-12~visualtest"
VERSION="1.0.3-12~visualtest12repair1"
PACKAGE="com.duonavi.app"
INJECT="var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries"
SOURCE_PLIST=ROOT/"runtime/visualtest12/DFVisual12GateRepair.plist"
LIB="DFVisual12GateRepair.dylib"
PLIST="DFVisual12GateRepair.plist"
BUNDLES={"com.apple.springboard","com.apple.CarPlayApp",
         "com.duoface.duofaceui","com.duonavi.app"}

def sha(data:bytes)->str:
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()

def run(*args:object)->None:
    subprocess.run([str(x) for x in args],check=True)

def dpkg_field(path:Path,key:str)->str:
    return subprocess.check_output(
        ["dpkg-deb","-f",str(path),key],text=True).strip()

def files(path:Path)->dict[str,str]:
    return {p.relative_to(path).as_posix():sha(p.read_bytes())
            for p in path.rglob("*") if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink()}

def symlinks(path:Path)->dict[str,str]:
    return {p.relative_to(path).as_posix():str(p.readlink())
            for p in path.rglob("*") if p.is_symlink()}

def main()->None:
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--dylib",required=True,type=Path)
    ap.add_argument("--out",required=True,type=Path)
    ap.add_argument("--report",required=True,type=Path)
    args=ap.parse_args()
    assert SOURCE.is_file() and sha(SOURCE.read_bytes())==PIN, "wrong VisualTest12 original"
    assert dpkg_field(SOURCE,"Package")==PACKAGE
    assert dpkg_field(SOURCE,"Version")==OLD
    assert dpkg_field(SOURCE,"Architecture")=="iphoneos-arm64"
    assert args.dylib.is_file() and args.dylib.stat().st_size>4000
    dylib=args.dylib.read_bytes()
    assert dylib[:4]==bytes.fromhex("cffaedfe"),"expected signed arm64 Mach-O dylib"
    cfg=plistlib.loads(SOURCE_PLIST.read_bytes())
    assert set(cfg["Filter"]["Bundles"])==BUNDLES
    assert args.out.resolve()!=SOURCE.resolve() and not args.out.exists()
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="visual12-gate-repair-") as temp:
        stage=Path(temp)/"stage"
        run("dpkg-deb","-R",SOURCE,stage)
        before=files(stage)
        old_symlinks=symlinks(stage)
        targets={f"{INJECT}/{LIB}",f"{INJECT}/{PLIST}"}
        assert not (targets & set(before))
        dest=stage/INJECT
        dest.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        (dest/LIB).write_bytes(dylib)
        (dest/PLIST).write_bytes(SOURCE_PLIST.read_bytes())
        control=stage/"DEBIAN/control"
        ctrl=control.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        pattern=f"Version: {OLD}\n"
        assert ctrl.count(pattern)==1
        control.write_text(ctrl.replace(pattern,f"Version: {VERSION}\n"),encoding="utf-8")
        md5=[]
        for path in sorted(stage.rglob("*")):
            if path.is_file() and not path.is_symlink():
                rel=path.relative_to(stage).as_posix()
                if not rel.startswith("DEBIAN/"):
                    md5.append(hashlib.md5(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()+"  "+rel)
        (stage/"DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("\n".join(md5)+"\n")
        after=files(stage)
        assert symlinks(stage)==old_symlinks
        assert not (set(before)-set(after)), "original file removed"
        new_files=set(after)-set(before)
        assert new_files-targets <= {"DEBIAN/md5sums"},new_files
        assert new_files & targets == targets
        modified=sorted(k for k in before if after[k]!=before[k])
        assert set(modified)<= {"DEBIAN/control","DEBIAN/md5sums"} and "DEBIAN/control" in modified
        preserved=[k for k in before if k.startswith("var/jb/")]
        assert preserved and all(before[k]==after[k] for k in preserved)
        args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        run("dpkg-deb","--build","--root-owner-group",stage,args.out)
    assert dpkg_field(args.out,"Package")==PACKAGE
    assert dpkg_field(args.out,"Version")==VERSION
    assert dpkg_field(args.out,"Architecture")=="iphoneos-arm64"
    report={
        "status":"VISUALTEST12_FUNCTIONAL_LICENSE_GATE_CANDIDATE_NOT_DEVICE_PROVEN",
        "package":PACKAGE,"version":VERSION,"original_source_sha256":PIN,
        "source_version":OLD,
        "added_files":sorted(targets),"changed_files":modified,
        "removed_original_files":[],
        "all_original_payload_files_byte_identical":True,
        "entire_original_scene_bridge_and_lifecycle_unmodified":True,
        "module":"runtime/visualtest12/DFVisual12GateRepair.m",
        "patched_getters":["CBLicenseSyncManager isLicenseCurrentlyValid",
                           "CBLicenseSyncManager remainingLicenseSeconds",
                           "CBLicenseSyncManager actualLicenseExpirationTimestamp"],
        "total_original_methods_neutralized":0,
        "duodash_binary_bundled":False,"airaw_binary_bundled":False,
        "hub_changed":False,"only_logging_probe":False,
        "device_tested":False,"sileo_published":False,
        "fallback_if_hooks_dont_load":"original behavior (unmodified source core)",
        "recovery_proven":False,
        "new_module_sha256":sha(dylib),
        "output_sha256":sha(args.out.read_bytes()),
        "output_bytes":args.out.stat().st_size,
    }
    args.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    args.report.write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False)+"\n")
    print(json.dumps(report,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False))

if __name__=="__main__":
    main()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Stage exact VisualTest12 launchfix1 + a READ-ONLY connection telemetry dylib.

This is not a repair to activation, licence, CarBridge or screen detection.
The original core must remain byte-exact; never touches independent Hub.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import plistlib
import subprocess
import tempfile

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
ORIGINAL=ROOT/"dist/visualtest12-launchfix1/com.duonavi.app_1.0.3-12~visualtest12launch1_iphoneos-arm64.deb"
PIN="e30148affc767ce039040f0e0cccd9a0ecbd40e523ee66409ca96e6ef2085f02"
FILTER=ROOT/"runtime/visualtest12/DFVisual12ConnectionProbe.plist"
PKG="com.duonavi.app"
OLD_VERSION="1.0.3-12~visualtest12launch1"
VERSION="1.0.3-12~visualtest12launch2probe1"
TARGET="var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries"
LIB="DFVisual12ConnectionProbe.dylib"
PLIST="DFVisual12ConnectionProbe.plist"

def sha(b:bytes)->str:return hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest()
def run(*cmd)->None:subprocess.run(list(map(str,cmd)),check=True)
def field(p:Path,key:str)->str:
    return subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb","-f",str(p),key],text=True).strip()
def all_files(p:Path)->dict[str,str]:
    return {f.relative_to(p).as_posix():sha(f.read_bytes()) for f in p.rglob("*")
            if f.is_file() and not f.is_symlink()}

def main()->None:
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--dylib",type=Path,required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--out",type=Path,required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--report",type=Path,required=True)
    args=ap.parse_args()
    assert sha(ORIGINAL.read_bytes())==PIN
    assert field(ORIGINAL,"Package")==PKG
    assert field(ORIGINAL,"Version")==OLD_VERSION
    assert field(ORIGINAL,"Architecture")=="iphoneos-arm64"
    assert args.dylib.is_file() and args.dylib.stat().st_size>4000
    dylib=args.dylib.read_bytes()
    assert dylib[:4]==bytes.fromhex("cffaedfe"),"expected generic ARM64 signed Mach-O dylib"
    filters=plistlib.loads(FILTER.read_bytes())
    bundles=filters["Filter"]["Bundles"]
    assert set(bundles)=={"com.apple.springboard","com.duoface.duofaceui","com.duonavi.app"}
    assert args.out!=ORIGINAL and not args.out.exists()
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="visual12-connect-probe-") as temp:
        root=Path(temp)/"stage"
        run("dpkg-deb","-R",ORIGINAL,root)
        before=all_files(root)
        for entry in (LIB,PLIST):
            assert (TARGET+"/"+entry) not in before
        loc=root/TARGET
        (loc/LIB).write_bytes(dylib)
        (loc/PLIST).write_bytes(FILTER.read_bytes())
        control=root/"DEBIAN/control"
        data=control.read_text()
        assert "Package: "+PKG+"\n" in data
        assert "Version: "+OLD_VERSION+"\n" in data
        control.write_text(data.replace("Version: "+OLD_VERSION+"\n",
                                        "Version: "+VERSION+"\n"))
        md5=[]
        for f in sorted(root.rglob("*")):
            if f.is_file() and not f.is_symlink() and f.relative_to(root).parts[0]!="DEBIAN":
                md5.append(hashlib.md5(f.read_bytes()).hexdigest()+"  "+f.relative_to(root).as_posix())
        (root/"DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("\n".join(md5)+"\n")
        after=all_files(root)
        added=sorted(set(after)-set(before))
        removed=sorted(set(before)-set(after))
        changed=sorted(k for k in before if k in after and before[k]!=after[k])
        assert not removed,removed
        assert added==sorted((TARGET+"/"+LIB,TARGET+"/"+PLIST)),added
        assert changed==["DEBIAN/control","DEBIAN/md5sums"],changed
        assert all(before[k]==after[k] for k in before
                   if k.startswith("var/jb/Applications/") or
                      k.endswith("DuoFaceDashboard.dylib") or
                      k.endswith("DuoFaceVisualHook.dylib")), "original core changed"
        args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        run("dpkg-deb","--build","--root-owner-group",root,args.out)
    assert field(args.out,"Package")==PKG
    assert field(args.out,"Version")==VERSION
    assert field(args.out,"Architecture")=="iphoneos-arm64"
    result={
       "status":"VISUALTEST12_CONNECTION_PROBE_PRESERVES_ALL_EXISTING_CORE_BYTES",
       "source_sha256":PIN,"package":PKG,"version":VERSION,
       "source_version":OLD_VERSION,
       "added_files":added,"changed_files":changed,
       "removed_files":removed,"unchanged_original_core":True,
       "all_prior_payload_byte_identical_except_control_md5":True,
       "new_dylib_sha256":sha(dylib),"new_filter_sha256":sha(FILTER.read_bytes()),
       "filter_bundles":bundles,
       "read_only_telemetry":True,
       "writes":"/var/mobile/Documents/DuoFaceConnectionProbe/{SpringBoard,DuoFaceUI}.jsonl",
       "separate_hub_modified":False,
       "physical_carplay_connection_fixed":False,
       "license_completely_removed":False,
       "device_tested":False,"sileo_published":False,

       "output_deb_bytes":args.out.stat().st_size,

########## BEGIN FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_visualtest12_launchfix1.py ##########
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Build actual com.duonavi.app VisualTest12 launch recovery test candidate.

Source is EXACT original VisualTest12 DEB, never the independent Hub binary.
Apply previously device-tested Native4 291 license method stubs and the
Native5 two expiry-state methods to its own ARM64 slices. Preserve all other
original package files including original CarPlay hook and asset bundle.
This is an installable recovery TEST, not runtime-proven/full-source rewrite.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import struct
import subprocess
import tempfile

from audit_native4_license_code import inspect, slurp_slices
from build_native4 import eligible, original_abi_stub, NOP
from build_native5 import FLAG_METHODS, preserve_entitlements_and_resign

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
SOURCE = ROOT / "dist/visual-1.0.3-12/com.duoface.app_1.0.3-12_visualtest_iphoneos-arm64.deb"
SOURCE_SHA = "8b7dedf77b9ec05465b83fdb6e2d7127800eac232a34ecf12f2c08b3983a481a"
VERSION = "1.0.3-12~visualtest12launch1"
PACKAGE = "com.duonavi.app"
FILES = {
    "DuoFaceUI": "var/jb/Applications/DuoFaceUI.app/DuoFaceUI",
    "DuoFaceDashboard": "var/jb/Library/MobileSubstrate/DynamicLibraries/DuoFaceDashboard.dylib",
}
EXPECTED_COUNTS = {"DuoFaceUI": 248, "DuoFaceDashboard": 43}

def digest(blob: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(blob).hexdigest()

def getfield(package: Path, key: str) -> str:
    return subprocess.check_output(["dpkg-deb", "-f", str(package), key], text=True).strip()

def write_patched_arm64(original: bytes, name: str) -> tuple[bytes, list[dict], list[dict], dict]:
    slices = list(slurp_slices(original))
    generic = [x for x in slices if x["arch"] == "arm64:0"]
    assert len(generic) == 1, (name, [s["arch"] for s in slices])
    raw = bytearray(generic[0]["bytes"])
    inventory = inspect(bytes(raw))
    assert not inventory["parser_warnings"], (name, inventory["parser_warnings"])
    assert not inventory["methods_without_safe_text_start"], (name, inventory["methods_without_safe_text_start"][:7])
    selected, expired, spans = [], [], []
    found_flags = set()
    for method in inventory["license_named_methods"]:
        key = (method["class"], method["selector"])
        is_license_method = eligible(*key)
        is_expiry_state_method = name == "DuoFaceUI" and key in FLAG_METHODS
        if not (is_license_method or is_expiry_state_method):
            continue
        assert method["listed_in_lc_function_starts"] and method["inside_executable_text"], key
        off = int(method["imp_file_offset"], 16)
        size = method["estimated_function_span"]
        assert isinstance(size, int) and 4 <= size < 100000 and size % 4 == off % 4 == 0, key
        if is_expiry_state_method:
            expected_sig, stub = FLAG_METHODS[key]
            assert method["type_encoding"] == expected_sig, (key, method["type_encoding"])
            found_flags.add(key)
        else:
            stub = original_abi_stub(method)
        assert 0 < len(stub) <= size and len(stub) % 4 == 0
        spans.append((off, off + size, key))
        original_span = bytes(raw[off:off + size])
        assert len(original_span) == size
        result = {
            "class": method["class"],
            "selector": method["selector"],
            "signature": method["type_encoding"],
            "offset_in_original_arm64": off, "size": size,
            "before_sha256": digest(original_span),
            "first_32_before_hex": original_span[:32].hex(),
            "stub_hex": stub.hex(),
        }
        if is_expiry_state_method:
            expired.append(result)
        else:
            selected.append(result)
        raw[off:off + size] = stub + NOP * ((size - len(stub)) // 4)
    spans.sort()
    for last, now in zip(spans, spans[1:]):
        assert last[1] <= now[0], ("overlap", name, last, now)
    assert len(selected) == EXPECTED_COUNTS[name], (name, len(selected), EXPECTED_COUNTS[name])
    assert len(expired) == (2 if name == "DuoFaceUI" else 0), (name, expired)
    assert found_flags == (set(FLAG_METHODS) if name == "DuoFaceUI" else set())
    after = inspect(bytes(raw))
    assert (after["method_count_license_related"]
            == inventory["method_count_license_related"])
    assert [m["class"] for m in after["license_named_methods"]] == [
        m["class"] for m in inventory["license_named_methods"]
    ], name
    return bytes(raw), selected, expired, {
        "source_archs": [s["arch"] for s in slices],
        "target_archs": ["arm64"],
        "related_class_method_metadata_retained": True,
        "source_raw_host_sha256": digest(original),
        "source_arm64_sha256": digest(generic[0]["bytes"]),
    }

def main() -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--out", required=True, type=Path)
    ap.add_argument("--report", required=True, type=Path)
    args = ap.parse_args()
    assert SOURCE.is_file() and digest(SOURCE.read_bytes()) == SOURCE_SHA
    assert getfield(SOURCE, "Package") == PACKAGE
    assert getfield(SOURCE, "Version") == "1.0.3-12~visualtest"
    assert getfield(SOURCE, "Architecture") == "iphoneos-arm64"
    assert args.out != SOURCE and not args.out.exists(), "Do not overwrite baseline"

    args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="visual12-launch1-") as dirname:
        root = Path(dirname)
        stage = root / "stage"
        subprocess.run(["dpkg-deb", "-R", str(SOURCE), str(stage)], check=True)
        before = {p.relative_to(stage).as_posix(): digest(p.read_bytes())
                  for p in stage.rglob("*") if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink()}
        result = {}
        for name, rel in FILES.items():
            file = stage / rel
            original = file.read_bytes()
            patched, methods, flags, meta = write_patched_arm64(original, name)
            # ldid signs only generic ARM64; the previously unmodified arm64e
            # slice is intentionally not shipped and must not run stale logic.
            file.write_bytes(patched)
            meta.update({
                "methods_neutralized": len(methods),
                "expired_flags_neutralized": len(flags),
                "patched_methods": methods,
                "expired_flags": flags,
                "entitlements": preserve_entitlements_and_resign(
                    file, original, root, name),
                "signed_host_sha256": digest(file.read_bytes()),
            })
            assert len(file.read_bytes()) >= len(patched)
            result[name] = meta
        control = stage / "DEBIAN/control"
        original_control = control.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        assert "Package: com.duonavi.app\n" in original_control
        assert "Version: 1.0.3-12~visualtest\n" in original_control
        control.write_text(original_control.replace(
            "Version: 1.0.3-12~visualtest\n",
            "Version: " + VERSION + "\n"), encoding="utf-8")
        md5lines = []
        for f in sorted(stage.rglob("*")):
            if f.is_file() and not f.is_symlink() and f.relative_to(stage).parts[0] != "DEBIAN":
                md5lines.append(hashlib.md5(f.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
                                + "  " + f.relative_to(stage).as_posix())
        (stage / "DEBIAN/md5sums").write_text("\n".join(md5lines) + "\n")
        after = {p.relative_to(stage).as_posix(): digest(p.read_bytes())
                 for p in stage.rglob("*") if p.is_file() and not p.is_symlink()}
        removed = set(before) - set(after)
        added = set(after) - set(before)
        # Original VisualTest12 package may omit DEBIAN/md5sums; generating
        # its standard manifest is allowed but removing any original is not.
        assert not removed, ("Original payload removed", removed)
        assert added <= {"DEBIAN/md5sums"}, ("Unexpected added payload", added)
        changed = sorted(k for k in after if before.get(k) != after[k])
        expected = sorted(list(FILES.values()) + ["DEBIAN/control", "DEBIAN/md5sums"])
        assert changed == expected, (changed, expected)
        assert sum(x["methods_neutralized"] for x in result.values()) == 291
        assert sum(x["expired_flags_neutralized"] for x in result.values()) == 2
        subprocess.run(["dpkg-deb", "--build", "--root-owner-group",
                        str(stage), str(args.out)], check=True)

    assert getfield(args.out, "Package") == PACKAGE
    assert getfield(args.out, "Version") == VERSION
    assert getfield(args.out, "Architecture") == "iphoneos-arm64"
    report = {
        "status": "VISUALTEST12_ORIGINAL_PACKAGE_LAUNCH_RECOVERY_SIGNED_DEB_CANDIDATE",
        "original_visualtest12_sha256": SOURCE_SHA,
        "package": PACKAGE, "version": VERSION, "arch": "iphoneos-arm64",
        "patched": result, "changed_original_paths": changed,
        "untouched_file_count": sum(before[k] == after[k] for k in before),
        "manifest_newly_created_if_missing": "DEBIAN/md5sums" not in before,
        "original_visualhook_files_unchanged": all(
            before[k] == after[k] for k in before
            if "DuoFaceVisualHook" in k),
        "source_original_package_retained": True,
        "hub_modified": False,
        "legacy_class_and_selector_metadata_retained": True,
        "not_proven_every_license_dependency_physically_removed": True,
        "launch_test_on_expired_device": "PENDING_REAL_DEVICE",
        "carplay_device_test": "PENDING_REAL_DEVICE",
        "sileo_published": False,
        "rollback_source_sha256": SOURCE_SHA,
        "output_sha256": digest(args.out.read_bytes()),
        "output_bytes": args.out.stat().st_size,
    }
    args.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    args.report.write_text(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n")
    print(json.dumps({
        "status": report["status"], "package": PACKAGE, "version": VERSION,
        "patched": {k: {"license_methods": len(v["patched_methods"]),
                        "expiry_flags": len(v["expired_flags"])}
                    for k, v in result.items()},
        "deb_sha256": report["output_sha256"],
        "device_launch_not_yet_tested": True,
    }, indent=2))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/build_visualtest12_launchfix1.py ##########

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Check actual Native12 ABI and Speed6Probe read-only single-renderer regression."""
from pathlib import Path
import json
def main():
    r=Path(".")
    source=(r/"runtime/speedbubble/DFSpeedNativeHostProbe.mm").read_text()
    previous=(r/"runtime/speedbubble/DFSpeedNativeSplitHost.mm").read_text()
    mk=(r/"Makefile.speedhud-split6-probe-stage").read_text()
    plist=(r/"DuoFaceSpeedHUDSplit6.plist").read_text()
    pack=(r/"migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native12_speed6probe1_candidate.py").read_text()
    native=json.loads((r/"audit/speedbubble/NATIVE12_GOFAHUD_SPLIT_SCENE_OWNER_READONLY.json").read_text())
    assert native["classes"]["CBWindow"]["+activeCarPlayWindow"]["objc_type"]=="@16@0:8"
    assert native["classes"]["CBBridgeManagerDashboard"]["-activated"]["objc_type"]=="B16@0:8"
    assert native["classes"]["CBBridgeManagerDashboard"]["-bridgeDidActivate:"]["objc_type"]=="v24@0:8@16"
    assert native["classes"]["CBWindow"]["-hide:"]["objc_type"]=="v24@0:8@?16"
    assert source.split("// Speed6: NEW renderer")[0]==previous.split("// Speed6: NEW renderer")[0]
    for code in ("S6DashboardActive()","S6OriginalGOFARendererDisabled()",
          "S6VerifiedHost(owner)","[host addSubview:s6HUD]",
          "[host bringSubviewToFront:s6HUD]","S6Unmount"):
        assert code in source,code
    for probe in ("PROBE_NATIVE_HOST","raw_active_window_class","raw_is_CBWindow",
          "host_probe_reason","root_scene_role","content_window_matches_root",
          "controller_window_matches_root","content_descends_from_controller",
          "hooks","s6ProbePrevious","now-s6ProbeAt<15.0"):
        assert probe in source,probe
    for forbidden in ("DFProbeCarPlayHostWindows","windowLevel=10000000",
         "initWithWindowScene:","connectedScenes","local.gofahud.carplay.show"):
        assert forbidden not in source,forbidden
    assert 'NSBundle.mainBundle.bundleIdentifier.lowercaseString' in source
    assert '@"com.apple.carplayapp"' in source
    assert "DFSpeedNativeHostProbe.mm runtime/speedbubble/DFSpeedTransport.mm" in mk
    assert "DFSpeedNativeSplitHost.mm" not in mk and "DFSpeedSceneOverlay" not in mk
    assert 'Bundles = ("com.apple.CarPlayApp")' in plist and "SpringBoard" not in plist
    assert 'NEW=("DuoFaceSpeedHUD.dylib","DuoFaceSpeedHUD.plist")' in pack
    assert 'shutil.copy2("DuoFaceSpeedHUDSplit6.plist",plist)' in pack
    assert 'assert dll.stem==plist.stem' in pack
    assert 'VERSION="0.1.0~native12+speed6zprobe1"' in pack
    assert '"host_probe_only_no_gate_relaxation":True' in pack
    assert '"scene_overlay_window_created":False' in pack
    print("SPEED6PROBE_NATIVE12_ABI_SINGLE_SPLIT_HOST_AND_LOG_SAFETY_STATIC_PASS_NOT_DEVICE_TESTED")
if __name__=="__main__":
    main()

"""Speed6 static safety regression; NOT proof of CarPlay runtime visibility."""
from pathlib import Path
import json

def main():
    root=Path(".")
    mm=(root/"runtime/speedbubble/DFSpeedNativeSplitHost.mm").read_text()
    make=(root/"Makefile.speedhud-split6-stage").read_text()
    plist=(root/"DuoFaceSpeedHUDSplit6.plist").read_text()

    assert methods["CBWindow"]["+activeCarPlayWindow"]["objc_type"]=="@16@0:8"

    assert "GOFAHUD" not in plist

    assert 'shutil.copy2("DuoFaceSpeedHUDSplit6.plist",plist)' in pack

    assert '"no_third_party_injection_or_waze_hooks":True' in pack
    assert "0.1.0~native12+speed6split" in pack
    assert "DuoFaceSpeedHUDSplit6.plist" in pack
    print("SPEED6_SPLIT_HOST_NATIVE12_ABI_AND_ONE_RENDERER_STATIC_PASS; NOT_DEVICE_TESTED")

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Speed7 QA: Native12 ABI, signed split root+2 real attached pane gate, zero extra UIWindow."""
from pathlib import Path
import json
r=Path(".")
s=(r/"runtime/speedbubble/DFSpeedNativeSplit7SpringBoard.mm").read_text()
original=(r/"runtime/speedbubble/DFSpeedNativeSplitHost.mm").read_text()
mk=(r/"Makefile.speedhud-native7split-stage").read_text()
pl=(r/"DuoFaceSpeedHUDNative7.plist").read_text()
pkg=(r/"migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native12_speed7split_candidate.py").read_text()
audit=json.loads((r/"audit/speedbubble/NATIVE12_SPEED4_SPLIT_OWNER_CHAIN_READONLY.json").read_text())
assert s.split("// Speed7: Native12 SpringBoard in-place split host")[0].strip().replace(
    "// Speed7: same visual HUD implementation, now hosted in native SpringBoard split view.",
    "// Inject this module into the verified DuoFace CarPlay process only; no global SpringBoard HUD.",
) == original.split("// Speed6: NEW renderer targeting")[0].strip()
for cls,sel,kind in [
 ("CBBridgeManager","+sharedInstanceIfExists","@16@0:8"),
 ("CBBridgeManager","-window","@16@0:8"),
 ("CBBridgeManager","-currentApp","@16@0:8"),
 ("CBWindow","-rootWindow","@16@0:8"),
 ("CBWindow","-isVisible","B16@0:8"),
]:
    # -currentApp was additionally observed in pinned lifecycle audit workflow.
    if sel in audit["methods"][cls]:
        assert audit["methods"][cls][sel]["objc_type"]==kind,(cls,sel)
    else: assert sel=="-currentApp" # Native12 split-lifecycle verified in CI output 38999369950.
for required in (
 "CBBridgeManager","sharedInstanceIfExists","CBBridgedSplitApp","currentApp",
 "appViews","count<2 || count>4","attached<2",
 "S7Ancestor(pane,splitRoot)","pane.window==root",
 "splitRoot.window!=root","root.screen==UIScreen.mainScreen",
 "CBTouchPassThroughRootSceneWindow","[controller addSubview:s7HUD]",
 "[controller bringSubviewToFront:s7HUD]","S7Unmount",
 "SPLIT7_MOUNTED_ONE_NATIVE_HOST","DuoFaceSpeedSplit7-SpringBoard.jsonl",
):
    assert required in s,required
for forbidden in ("[[UIWindow alloc]","initWithWindowScene:","windowLevel=10000000",
 "connectedScenes","method_setImplementation","MSHookMessageEx",
 "local.gofahud.carplay.show","DuoFaceSpeedScene"):
    assert forbidden not in s,forbidden
assert "DFSpeedNativeSplit7SpringBoard.mm runtime/speedbubble/DFSpeedTransport.mm" in mk
assert 'Executables = ("SpringBoard")' in pl and "CarPlayApp" not in pl
assert 'VERSION="0.1.0~native12+speed7split"' in pkg
assert 'PIN="d63cf64a5139c7c235b9836b963f2a6a9f25ac414c4eda79bcea07ddda32475a"' in pkg
assert 'assert len(before)==43' in pkg
assert '"replaced_only_HUD_dylib_and_plist":True' in pkg
assert '"creates_new_UIWindow":False' in pkg
print("SPEED7_NATIVE12_SINGLE_SPLIT_PANE_GATE_41_BYTE_IDENTICAL_STATIC_PASS_NOT_DEVICE_TESTED")

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Speed8 static regression contract. Real Mac Theos build and device test are separate gates."""
from pathlib import Path
p=Path(".")
src=(p/"runtime/speedbubble/DFSpeedNativeSplit8SpringBoard.mm").read_text()
make=(p/"Makefile.speedhud-native8compact-stage").read_text()
reader=(p/"runtime/speedbubble/DFSpeedGMapReader8.mm").read_text()
transport=(p/"runtime/speedbubble/DFSpeedTransport8.mm").read_text()
builder=(p/"migration/full_payload/native_test/build_native12_speed8compact_candidate.py").read_text()
plist=(p/"DuoFaceSpeedHUDNative7.plist").read_text()
for x in ("CBBridgeManager","CBWindow","CBBridgedSplitApp","currentApp",
          "count<2 || count>4","attached<2","pane.window==root",
          "CBTouchPassThroughRootSceneWindow","S7Unmount",
          "[root addSubview:s7HUD]","[root bringSubviewToFront:s7HUD]",
          "DFAllowedArea(s7HUD)","initWithFrame:CGRectMake(0,0,104,46)",
          "UILongPressGestureRecognizer","showSettings:",
          "settingsAction:","DuoFaceSpeedSplit8-SpringBoard.jsonl"):
    assert x in src,x
assert "CGRect area=parent.bounds;" in src
assert "s7HUD.superview==root && s7HUD.window==root" in src
for x in ("[[UIWindow alloc]","windowLevel=10000000","initWithWindowScene:",
          "connectedScenes","method_setImplementation","MSHookMessageEx",
          "local.gofahud.carplay.show"):
    assert x not in src,x
assert "DFSpeedNativeSplit8SpringBoard.mm" in make
assert "DFSpeedTransport8.mm" in make and "DFSpeedGMapReader8.mm" in make
assert "-DDF_SPEED_HUD_SPRINGBOARD=1" in make
assert "Executables = (\"SpringBoard\")" in plist
assert "RequestPinnedGMapSource" in reader and "com.chou.gmaphud.source.selection" in reader
assert "selectedSource" in src and '@"source"' in reader
assert "DFSpeed::ReadPinnedGMapWire(native)" in transport
assert "if (pinned && src!=pinned)" in transport
assert 'PIN="a59c65f96457b436a0adf581ce96ad0a7fd769552f13ca67112b41b525811155"' in builder
assert 'VERSION="0.1.0~native12+speed8compact"' in builder
assert 'REPLACE=("DuoFaceSpeedHUD.dylib",)' in builder
assert "assert len(originals)==42" in builder
assert '"data_feed_live_on_device_verified":False' in builder
print("SPEED8_STATIC_DRAG_SETTINGS_GMAP_READER_AND_42_UNCHANGED_PAYLOAD_PASS_NOT_DEVICE_TESTED")
########## END FILE: migration/full_payload/native_test/check_native12_speed8compact_contract.py ##########
